📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在 258 名暴露的参与者中,与未接触过的受试者相比,接触过的受试者对两种菌株表现出更高的 HI 几何平均滴度,接触过的受试者的血清转化率超过 89%,并且反应持续到第 202 天;引起的不良事件为轻度至中度,没有发生与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。
  • 与 WHO 推荐的候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV)、AL/39 相比,cHANA 在小鼠体内引发了更有效的交叉反应抗体反应和 T 细胞免疫。它还引发了针对异源 H9N2 病毒致命攻击的有效交叉保护,并显着降低了小鼠的肺部病毒载量。
  • 1591 种 H9N2 病毒共鉴定出 52 种基因型,其中新定义基因型 48 种(G118~G163),其中 G57 和 G118 为主要流行基因型。贝叶斯分析揭示了 12 条迁徙路径,并确定华南地区是 传播 的主要中心。
  • 神经病理学显示所有 4 只动物均患有淋巴组织细胞性至混合性中性粒细胞性脑膜脑炎,并伴有神经元坏死、神经吞噬、神经胶质细胞增殖、多灶性出血和血管周围套扎。免疫组织化学检测到神经元(3/3)、神经胶质细胞(3/3)和室管膜细胞(2/3)中的病毒核蛋白。新发现包括脉络丛炎(2/4)和脊髓炎(2/4)。全身性病变包括象海豹的多灶性坏死性心肌炎和海狮胎儿组织中检测到的高致病性禽流感病毒引起的多灶性坏死性胎盘炎。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Among 258 exposed participants, primed subjects showed higher HI geometric mean titers against both strains than unprimed subjects, with seroconversion rates exceeding 89% in primed groups and responses persisting to Day 202; solicited adverse events were mild to moderate and no vaccine-related serious adverse events occurred.
  • Compared to the WHO-recommended candidate vaccine virus (CVV), AL/39, cHANA elicited more potent cross-reactive antibody responses and T cell immunity in mice.
  • A total of 52 genotypes were identified among 1,591 H9N2 viruses, including 48 newly defined genotypes (G118–G163), with G57 and G118 as the main epidemic genotypes.
  • Neuropathology revealed lymphohistiocytic to mixed neutrophilic meningoencephalitis with neuronal necrosis, neuronophagia, glial cell proliferation, multifocal hemorrhage, and perivascular cuffing in all 4 animals.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Human Vaccines & Immunotherapeutics  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-05  |  DOI: 10.1080/21645515.2026.2712791  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 22, 1, 2712791
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

针对 H5 禽流感 的异源初免增强 疫苗接种:MF59 佐剂、细胞培养衍生的 H5N6 疫苗 的安全性和免疫原性。

Heterologous prime-boost vaccination against H5 avian influenza: Safety and immunogenicity of a MF59-adjuvanted, cell-culture derived H5N6 vaccine.
作者： Esther van Twuijver, Eve Versage, Robert L. Jacobs, Matthew Hohenboken
摘要中文翻译随着全球报告的 H5 禽流感 病例的增加以及出现大流行的可能性,交叉反应性抗体反应的诱导可能代表了有效 疫苗 的一个重要属性。这项 2 期扩展研究评估了 MF59 佐剂、细胞培养物衍生的 H5N6 疫苗 (aH5N6c) 在用 MF59 佐剂、细胞培养物衍生的 H5N1 疫苗 (aH5N1c) 免疫的成人和未免疫成人中的免疫原性和安全性。先前在母研究 V89_18 中接受过两剂 aH5N1c 的成年人被随机接受两剂 aH5N6c 剂量(第 1 组)或一剂 aH5N6c 和一剂安慰剂(第 2 组),间隔 3 周。未接种疫苗的成年人接受两剂 aH5N6c 剂量(第 3 组)。在第 1、8、22、43 和 202 天,通过针对初免(H5N1)和加强(H5N6)菌株的血凝抑制(HI)和微中和(MN)测定来评估免疫原性。在 258 名暴露参与者中,初免受试者(第 1 组和第 2 组)对两种菌株的 HI 几何平均滴度高于未初免受试者(第 3 组)受试者,并有 MN 反应同样增强。异源 H5N1 反应在初免受试者中表现强劲(第 43 天 HI GMT:333-343;血清转化率 >89%),但在未初免受试者中反应最小,反应持续到第 202 天。所引起的不良事件为轻度或中度,组间可比,并且与其他 MF59 佐剂的流行病 疫苗 一致;没有发生与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。H5N1 引发的成人中的异源 H5N6 加强剂 疫苗接种 引发了针对引发菌株的强烈交叉反应免疫,证明了至少 6 年的持久免疫记忆,并支持针对新出现的 H5 暴发 的大流行防范的异源引发加强策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过评估异源初免-加强 疫苗接种 是否可以诱导针对抗原不同的 H5 毒株的交叉反应性抗体反应,解决了为潜在的 H5 禽流感 大流行做好准备的紧迫公共卫生需求。
设计与对象
这项 2 期扩展研究招募了之前接受过两剂 MF59 佐剂、细胞培养物衍生的 H5N1 疫苗 的成年人,并将他们随机分配接受两剂 H5N6 疫苗、一剂 H5N6 加安慰剂,或未接受注射的成年人接受两剂 H5N6。
核心方法
在第 1、8、22、43 和 202 天,通过针对初免 H5N1 和加强 H5N6 毒株的血凝抑制和微量中和测定来测量免疫原性;对引起的不良事件进行安全性监测。
主要结果
在 258 名暴露的参与者中,与未接触过的受试者相比,接触过的受试者对两种菌株表现出更高的 HI 几何平均滴度,接触过的受试者的血清转化率超过 89%,并且反应持续到第 202 天;引起的不良事件为轻度至中度,没有发生与 疫苗 相关的严重不良事件。
解释与创新
作者将初免成人中强烈的异源 H5N1 反应解释为持续至少六年的持久免疫记忆的证据,强调了异源初免-加强策略在针对新出现的 H5 暴发 的大流行防范方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在 H5N1 引发的人群中使用异源 H5N6 增强剂来快速扩大交叉反应免疫力,为大流行 疫苗 部署和针对 H5 禽流感 的 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的单中心设计、有限的随访时间以及缺乏许可的比较器限制了普遍性和长期安全性评估,需要对证据有一定的信心。

Heterologous prime-boost vaccination against H5 avian influenza: Safety and immunogenicity of a MF59-adjuvanted, cell-culture derived H5N6 vaccine.

Authors: Esther van Twuijver, Eve Versage, Robert L. Jacobs, Matthew Hohenboken
Original abstract (English)
With increasing H5 avian influenza cases reported globally and the potential for pandemic emergence, induction of cross-reactive antibody responses may represent an important attribute of an effective vaccine. This phase 2 extension study evaluated immunogenicity and safety of MF59-adjuvanted, cell culture-derived H5N6 vaccine (aH5N6c) in adults primed with MF59-adjuvanted, cell culture-derived H5N1 vaccine (aH5N1c) and in unprimed adults. Adults previously primed with two doses of aH5N1c in the parent study V89_18 were randomized to receive two aH5N6c doses (Group 1) or one aH5N6c and one placebo (Group 2) 3 weeks apart. Unprimed adults received two aH5N6c doses (Group 3). Immunogenicity was assessed by hemagglutination inhibition (HI) and microneutralization (MN) assays against the priming (H5N1) and booster (H5N6) strains on Days 1, 8, 22, 43, and 202. Among 258 exposed participants, primed subjects (Groups 1 and 2) showed higher HI geometric mean titers against both strains than unprimed (Group 3) subjects, with MN responses similarly enhanced. Heterologous H5N1 responses were robust in primed subjects (Day 43 HI GMTs: 333-343; seroconversion rates >89%) but minimal in unprimed subjects, with responses persisting to Day 202. Solicited adverse events were mild or moderate, comparable between groups, and consistent with other MF59-adjuvanted pandemic vaccines; no vaccine-related serious adverse events occurred. Heterologous H5N6 booster vaccination in H5N1-primed adults elicited strong cross-reactive immunity against the priming strain, demonstrating long-lasting immune memory for at least 6 y and supporting heterologous prime-boost strategies for pandemic preparedness against emerging H5 outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the urgent public-health need to prepare for potential H5 avian influenza pandemics by evaluating whether heterologous prime-boost vaccination could induce cross-reactive antibody responses against antigenically distinct H5 strains.
Design and population
This phase 2 extension study enrolled adults previously primed with two doses of MF59-adjuvanted, cell culture-derived H5N1 vaccine and randomized them to receive two H5N6 vaccine doses, one H5N6 dose plus placebo, or two H5N6 doses in unprimed adults.
Core methods
Immunogenicity was measured by hemagglutination inhibition and microneutralization assays against both the priming H5N1 and booster H5N6 strains on Days 1, 8, 22, 43, and 202; safety was monitored for solicited adverse events.
Main results
Among 258 exposed participants, primed subjects showed higher HI geometric mean titers against both strains than unprimed subjects, with seroconversion rates exceeding 89% in primed groups and responses persisting to Day 202; solicited adverse events were mild to moderate and no vaccine-related serious adverse events occurred.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the robust heterologous H5N1 responses in primed adults as evidence of durable immune memory lasting at least six years, highlighting the potential of heterologous prime-boost strategies for pandemic preparedness against emerging H5 outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the use of heterologous H5N6 boosting in H5N1-primed populations to rapidly broaden cross-reactive immunity, informing pandemic vaccine deployment and surveillance strategies for H5 avian influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s single-centre design, limited follow-up duration, and absence of a licensed comparator limit generalisability and long-term safety assessment, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

Journal: Emerging Microbes & Infections  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.1080/22221751.2026.2713325  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 2713325
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

掺入表位优化表面蛋白的二价灭活流感 疫苗 赋予针对 H9N2 流感病毒的交叉保护性免疫力。

A bivalent inactivated influenza vaccine incorporating epitope-optimized surface proteins confers cross-protective immunity against H9N2 influenza virus.
作者： Mengchan Hao, Yiwei Guan, Meng Xu, Wenxue Yang, Yanhai Wang, Yuan Zhang, Jianjun Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 H9N2 禽流感 病毒(AIV)给全球家禽业造成了重大经济损失,并对人类构成人畜共患威胁。疫苗接种 构成了预防和控制 H9N2 AIV 的关键策略。然而,该病毒持续存在的抗原性 进化 对现有 疫苗 的保护功效提出了持续的挑战。因此,开发能够引发交叉反应的 免疫应答 的广泛保护性 H9N2 流感 疫苗 对于减轻疾病负担和大流行风险至关重要。在这里,我们通过组合两种单独拯救的嵌合灭活病毒 cHANA1 和 cHANA2,开发了一种二价嵌合灭活 疫苗,命名为 cHANA。每种重组病毒都携带一组 Epigraph 设计的 HA 和 NA 免疫原,这两组免疫原根据全球 H9N2 HA 和 NA 序列数据集进行了计算优化,以在整个 H9N2 病毒群体的表位覆盖方面相互补充。与 WHO 推荐的候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV)、AL/39 相比,cHANA 在小鼠体内引发了更有效的交叉反应抗体反应和 T 细胞免疫。此外,它还能针对异源 H9N2 病毒的致命攻击产生有效的交叉保护,并显着降低小鼠的肺部病毒载量。通过赋予广泛的保护性免疫力,该 疫苗 代表了一种有前途的控制 H9N2 暴发 的通用候选药物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 H9N2 禽流感 病毒的持续抗原 进化 带来的挑战,该挑战破坏了现有 疫苗 的有效性。H9N2 禽流感 病毒给全球家禽业造成了重大经济损失,并对人类构成人畜共患威胁。
设计与对象
一种二价嵌合灭活病毒 疫苗,命名为 cHANA,是通过结合两种单独拯救的嵌合灭活病毒 cHANA1 和 cHANA2 开发的。该研究评估了 疫苗 在小鼠中对抗异源 H9N2 病毒致死攻击的功效。
核心方法
疫苗 是通过计算优化来自全球 H9N2 序列数据集的 HA 和 NA 免疫原而构建的。每种重组病毒都携带一组 Epigraph 设计的 HA 和 NA 免疫原,这两组免疫原经过优化,可在 H9N2 病毒群体的表位覆盖范围上相互补充。
主要结果
与 WHO 推荐的候选 疫苗 病毒 (CVV)、AL/39 相比,cHANA 在小鼠体内引发了更有效的交叉反应抗体反应和 T 细胞免疫。它还引发了针对异源 H9N2 病毒致命攻击的有效交叉保护,并显着降低了小鼠的肺部病毒载量。
解释与创新
结果表明,chHANA 疫苗 增强了针对 H9N2 流感病毒的交叉反应免疫力,这是开发针对这种不断进化的病原体的广泛保护性 疫苗 的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,chHANA 可能是一种有前途的通用 疫苗 候选药物,用于控制 H9N2 暴发,通过降低人畜共患 传播 的风险,为家禽业和公共卫生带来潜在益处。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,结果可能无法完全转化为人类。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它直接将 疫苗 的功效与 WHO 推荐的候选药物进行比较,并在相关动物模型中证明了交叉保护。

A bivalent inactivated influenza vaccine incorporating epitope-optimized surface proteins confers cross-protective immunity against H9N2 influenza virus.

Authors: Mengchan Hao, Yiwei Guan, Meng Xu, Wenxue Yang, Yanhai Wang, Yuan Zhang, Jianjun Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) has caused substantial economic losses to the global poultry industry and poses a zoonotic threat to humans. Vaccination constitutes a pivotal strategy for the prevention and control of H9N2 AIVs. However, the ongoing antigenic evolution of the virus poses a persistent challenge to the protective efficacy of existing vaccines. Therefore, the development of a broadly protective H9N2 influenza vaccine capable of eliciting cross-reactive immune responses is crucial for mitigating both the disease burden and the risk of pandemics. Here, we developed a bivalent chimeric inactivated vaccine, designated cHANA, by combining two individually rescued chimeric inactivated viruses, cHANA1 and cHANA2. Each recombinant virus carries one set of Epigraph-designed HA and NA immunogens, and the two sets were computationally optimized from global H9N2 HA and NA sequence datasets to complement each other in epitope coverage across the H9N2 viral population. Compared to the WHO-recommended candidate vaccine virus (CVV), AL/39, cHANA elicited more potent cross-reactive antibody responses and T cell immunity in mice. Furthermore, it elicited effective cross-protection against lethal challenge with heterologous H9N2 virus and significantly reduced pulmonary viral loads of mice. By conferring broad protective immunity, this vaccine represents a promising universal candidate for controlling H9N2 outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge posed by the ongoing antigenic evolution of H9N2 avian influenza virus, which undermines the effectiveness of existing vaccines. The H9N2 avian influenza virus has caused substantial economic losses to the global poultry industry and poses a zoonotic threat to humans.
Design and population
A bivalent chimeric inactivated vaccine, designated cHANA, was developed by combining two individually rescued chimeric inactivated viruses, cHANA1 and cHANA2. The study evaluated the vaccine's efficacy in mice against lethal challenge with heterologous H9N2 virus.
Core methods
The vaccine was constructed by computationally optimizing HA and NA immunogens from global H9N2 sequence datasets. Each recombinant virus carries one set of Epigraph-designed HA and NA immunogens, and the two sets were optimized to complement each other in epitope coverage across the H9N2 viral population.
Main results
Compared to the WHO-recommended candidate vaccine virus (CVV), AL/39, cHANA elicited more potent cross-reactive antibody responses and T cell immunity in mice. It also elicited effective cross-protection against lethal challenge with heterologous H9N2 virus and significantly reduced pulmonary viral loads of mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that the cHANA vaccine provides enhanced cross-reactive immunity against H9N2 influenza virus, which is a novel approach to developing a broadly protective vaccine against this evolving pathogen.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that cHANA could be a promising universal vaccine candidate for controlling H9N2 outbreaks, offering potential benefits for both poultry industry and public health by mitigating the risk of zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mouse models, and the results may not fully translate to human populations. However, the evidence is strong as it directly compares the vaccine's efficacy to a WHO-recommended candidate and demonstrates cross-protection in a relevant animal model.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2014年至2025年中国H9N2 进化病毒的基因进化、系统动力学、地理传播:潜在人畜共患病风险不断增加。

Genetic evolution, phylodynamics, geographic spread of H9N2 avian influenza viruses in China from 2014 to 2025: an increasing potential zoonotic risk.
作者： Zifeng Pang, Peiting Zhong, Cuishan Mai, Junkun Fan, Yejia Wu, Yao Zhang, Hanlin Liu, Hailiang Sun, Ming Liao
摘要中文翻译禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的 H9N2 亚型对家禽业和公共卫生构成越来越大的威胁。但自2014年以来,国内尚未对其基因进化、基因型、空间动态及致病性进行系统研究。因此,我们利用公共数据库和实验室分离株对2014年至2025年中国H9N2病毒的基因组进行了大规模序列分析。在本研究中,我们在 1,591 种 H9N2 病毒中鉴定出 52 种不同的基因型,包括 4 种先前识别的基因型(G6、G57、G58 和 G68)和 48 种新定义的基因型(G118-G163)。G57和G118是2014-2025年中国主要流行基因型。贝叶斯谱系地理学分析显示,2017-2022年H9N2禽流感在中国传播存在12条明显的迁徙路径,其中华南地区是传播的主要中心。H9N2 AIV 继续在中国的鸡和鸭中传播,并传播到其他 宿主。具有G57和G118基因型的H9N2 AIV可以在MDCK、CEF、A549和HBE细胞中有效复制,滴度为0.97-8.5 lgTCID 50 /mL。G57和G118基因型H9N2病毒优先与α-2,6连接的唾液酸糖聚合物(人类受体)结合,并在小鼠和鸡的多个器官中有效复制并引起肺部病理变化。因此,我国有必要加强H9N2禽流感病毒的监测和预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H9N2 进化 亚型病毒(AIV)对家禽和公共卫生构成越来越大的威胁,但自 2014 年以来,缺乏对其基因 进化、基因型动态、空间传播和致病性的系统分析。
设计与对象
该研究整合了公共数据库和实验室分离株,对 2014 年至 2025 年来自中国的 H9N2 AIV 基因组 进行了大规模序列分析。
核心方法
检索并分析 基因组 序列以定义基因型并进行贝叶斯系统发育地理学分析以重建迁移路径和 传播 中心。
主要结果
1591 种 H9N2 病毒共鉴定出 52 种基因型,其中新定义基因型 48 种(G118~G163),其中 G57 和 G118 为主要流行基因型。贝叶斯分析揭示了 12 条迁徙路径,并确定华南地区是 传播 的主要中心。
解释与创新
作者将 G57 和 G118 基因型的优势及其扩大的 宿主 范围解释为人畜共患潜力增加的证据,强调了增强 监测 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持中国(特别是华南地区)针对 H9N2 AIV 的针对性 监测 和预防策略,以减轻人畜共患和家禽业风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于公开可用的序列和实验室分离株,这可能不足以代表地理和时间多样性,限制了普遍性并引入了抽样偏差。

Genetic evolution, phylodynamics, geographic spread of H9N2 avian influenza viruses in China from 2014 to 2025: an increasing potential zoonotic risk.

Authors: Zifeng Pang, Peiting Zhong, Cuishan Mai, Junkun Fan, Yejia Wu, Yao Zhang, Hanlin Liu, Hailiang Sun, Ming Liao
Original abstract (English)
Subtype H9N2 of the avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a growing threat to the poultry industry and public health. However, since 2014, there has been no systematic study of its genetic evolution, genotype, spatial dynamics, and pathogenicity in China. Therefore, we performed a large-scale sequence analysis of the genome of Chinese H9N2 viruses from 2014 to 2025 using public databases and laboratory isolates. We identified 52 different genotypes in 1,591 H9N2 viruses, including 4 previously recognized genotypes (G6, G57, G58, and G68) and 48 newly defined genotypes (G118-G163) in this study. G57 and G118 were the main epidemic genotypes in China from 2014 to 2025. Bayesian phylogeographic analysis showed that there were 12 obvious migration paths for the spread of H9N2 AIVs in China from 2017 to 2022. In particular, the South China region was the main transmission centre. H9N2 AIV continues to circulate in chickens and ducks in China and spreads to other hosts. The H9N2 AIVs with the G57 and G118 genotypes could effectively replicate in MDCK, CEF, A549, and HBE cells with titres of 0.97-8.5 lgTCID 50 /mL. The G57 and G118 genotypes H9N2 viruses preferentially bound to α-2,6-linked sialic acid glycopolymers (human receptors), and effectively replicate in multiple organs of mice and chickens and cause pathological changes in the lungs. Thus, it is necessary to strengthen the monitoring and prevention of H9N2 AIVs in China.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Subtype H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a growing threat to poultry and public health, yet systematic analyses of its genetic evolution, genotype dynamics, spatial spread, and pathogenicity in China since 2014 were lacking.
Design and population
The study performed a large-scale sequence analysis of H9N2 AIV genomes from China spanning 2014 to 2025, integrating public databases and laboratory isolates.
Core methods
Genome sequences were retrieved and analyzed to define genotypes and perform Bayesian phylogeographic analysis to reconstruct migration paths and transmission centres.
Main results
A total of 52 genotypes were identified among 1,591 H9N2 viruses, including 48 newly defined genotypes (G118–G163), with G57 and G118 as the main epidemic genotypes. Bayesian analysis revealed 12 migration paths and identified South China as the main transmission centre.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the predominance of G57 and G118 genotypes and their expanded host range as evidence of increasing zoonotic potential, highlighting the need for enhanced surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted surveillance and prevention strategies for H9N2 AIV in China, particularly in South China, to mitigate zoonotic and poultry industry risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on publicly available sequences and laboratory isolates, which may underrepresent geographic and temporal diversity, limiting generalisability and introducing sampling bias.

阿根廷自然感染高致病性 禽流感 H5N1 病毒的南美鳍足类动物的神经病理学、新组织向性和垂直 传播 的证据。

Neuropathology, novel tissue tropism, and evidence of vertical transmission in South American pinnipeds naturally infected with high pathogenicity avian influenza H5N1 virus in Argentina.
作者： Carla Fiorito, Antonio Fernández, Ana Colom-Rivero, Paula Alonso-Almorox, Marisa Andrada, Daniel Marcelo Lombardo, Eva Sierra
摘要中文翻译2023 年,高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 暴发 引发了大规模 死亡率 事件,影响了整个南美洲的鳍足类动物。在这里,我们介绍了在阿根廷丘布特 暴发 期间搁浅的 3 只南美海狮 (Otaria flavescens) 和 1 只南象海豹 (Mirounga leonina) 与 HPAI H5N1 感染相关的临床、病理、免疫组织化学和分子发现。在两只海狮中观察到神经系统症状,其中一只还表现出呼吸困难。对所有 4 只动物进行尸检。神经病理学显示所有动物均患有轻度至重度淋巴组织细胞性(2/4)至混合性中性粒细胞性(2/4)脑膜脑炎,并伴有神经元坏死、神经吞噬、神经胶质细胞增殖、多灶性出血和血管周围套扎。HPAI H5N1 感染的鳍足类的新发现包括脉络丛炎 (2/4) 和脊髓炎 (2/4)。4 只动物中有 3 只的病毒核蛋白免疫组织化学呈阳性,涉及神经元 (3/3)、神经胶质细胞 (3/3) 和室管膜细胞 (2/3)。与病毒相关的全身性发现包括象海豹的多灶性坏死性心肌炎和海狮的多灶性坏死性胎盘炎,并在胎儿组织中检测到高致病性禽流感病毒。一只海狮的肺部病变很小,仅限于支气管腺体多灶性坏死。通过聚合酶链反应和测序在所有动物中证实了 HPAI H5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒。这些发现表明 HPAI H5N1 感染的鳍足类动物中枢神经系统受累占主导地位,并扩大了病毒相关病变的认识范围,确定了新的组织向性和 传播 途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决2023年高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 暴发对南美鳍足类动物的严重影响,该影响导致了大规模的死亡率事件。作者旨在描述自然感染的鳍足类 HPAI H5N1 感染的临床、病理和分子特征,以填补有关组织向性和 传播 途径的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究检查了在阿根廷丘布特省 HPAI H5N1 暴发 期间搁浅的 4 只鳍足类动物,其中包括 3 只南美海狮 (Otaria flavescens) 和 1 只南象海豹 (Mirounga leonina)。对所有动物进行尸检以评估临床和病理结果。
核心方法
使用聚合酶链式反应和测序在所有动物中证实了 HPAI H5 clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒感染。神经病理学检查包括脑膜脑炎、神经元坏死、神经胶质细胞增殖、出血和血管周围套扎的评估。
主要结果
神经病理学显示所有 4 只动物均患有淋巴组织细胞性至混合性中性粒细胞性脑膜脑炎,并伴有神经元坏死、神经吞噬、神经胶质细胞增殖、多灶性出血和血管周围套扎。免疫组织化学检测到神经元(3/3)、神经胶质细胞(3/3)和室管膜细胞(2/3)中的病毒核蛋白。新发现包括脉络丛炎(2/4)和脊髓炎(2/4)。全身性病变包括象海豹的多灶性坏死性心肌炎和海狮胎儿组织中检测到的高致病性禽流感病毒引起的多灶性坏死性胎盘炎。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPAI H5N1 感染的鳍足类动物主要受累于中枢神经系统,并扩大了病毒相关病变的认识范围。确定了新的组织向性和 传播 途径,包括垂直 传播。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要增强海洋哺乳动物 HPAI H5N1 的 监测 和诊断方法,以便为保护策略提供信息并评估人畜共患风险。它强调了监测受影响物种的神经和生殖系统参与情况的重要性。
局限与证据强度
样本量小(n = 4)和缺乏对照动物限制了因果推论的普遍性和强度。该研究依赖于尸检,可能无法捕获早期或亚临床感染,并且研究结果特定于单个 暴发 位置。

Neuropathology, novel tissue tropism, and evidence of vertical transmission in South American pinnipeds naturally infected with high pathogenicity avian influenza H5N1 virus in Argentina.

Authors: Carla Fiorito, Antonio Fernández, Ana Colom-Rivero, Paula Alonso-Almorox, Marisa Andrada, Daniel Marcelo Lombardo, Eva Sierra
Original abstract (English)
In 2023, high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 outbreaks caused mass mortality events affecting pinnipeds throughout South America. Here, we present the clinical, pathological, immunohistochemical, and molecular findings associated with HPAI H5N1 infection in 3 South American sea lions ( Otaria flavescens ) and 1 southern elephant seal ( Mirounga leonina ) stranded during the outbreak in Chubut, Argentina. Neurological signs were observed in 2 sea lions, with 1 also exhibiting respiratory distress. Necropsies were conducted on all 4 animals. Neuropathology revealed mild-to-severe lymphohistiocytic (2/4) to mixed neutrophilic (2/4) meningoencephalitis with neuronal necrosis, neuronophagia, glial cell proliferation, multifocal hemorrhage, and perivascular cuffing in all animals. Novel findings for HPAI H5N1-infected pinnipeds included choroid plexitis (2/4) and myelitis (2/4). Immunohistochemistry for viral nucleoprotein was positive in 3 of 4 animals and involved neurons (3/3), glial cells (3/3), and ependymal cells (2/3). Viral-related systemic findings included multifocal necrotizing myocarditis in the elephant seal and multifocal necrotizing placentitis in a sea lion, with HPAI virus detected within fetal tissues. Pulmonary lesions were minimal and were limited to multifocal necrosis of bronchial glands in 1 sea lion. HPAI H5 clade 2.3.4.4b virus was confirmed by polymerase chain reaction and sequencing in all animals. These findings indicate a predominance of central nervous system involvement in HPAI H5N1-infected pinnipeds and expand the recognized spectrum of virus-associated lesions, identifying novel tissue tropisms and transmission routes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the severe impact of high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 outbreaks on South American pinnipeds in 2023, which caused mass mortality events. The authors aimed to characterize the clinical, pathological, and molecular features of HPAI H5N1 infection in naturally infected pinnipeds to fill knowledge gaps about tissue tropism and transmission routes.
Design and population
The study examined 4 pinnipeds—3 South American sea lions (Otaria flavescens) and 1 southern elephant seal (Mirounga leonina)—stranded during an HPAI H5N1 outbreak in Chubut, Argentina. Necropsies were conducted on all animals to assess clinical and pathological findings.
Core methods
HPAI H5 clade 2.3.4.4b virus infection was confirmed using polymerase chain reaction and sequencing in all animals. Neuropathological examination included assessment of meningoencephalitis, neuronal necrosis, glial cell proliferation, hemorrhage, and perivascular cuffing.
Main results
Neuropathology revealed lymphohistiocytic to mixed neutrophilic meningoencephalitis with neuronal necrosis, neuronophagia, glial cell proliferation, multifocal hemorrhage, and perivascular cuffing in all 4 animals. Immunohistochemistry detected viral nucleoprotein in neurons (3/3), glial cells (3/3), and ependymal cells (2/3). Novel findings included choroid plexitis (2/4) and myelitis (2/4). Systemic lesions included multifocal necrotizing myocarditis in the elephant seal and multifocal necrotizing placentitis with HPAI virus detected in fetal tissues of a sea lion.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate a predominance of central nervous system involvement in HPAI H5N1-infected pinnipeds and expand the recognized spectrum of virus-associated lesions. Novel tissue tropisms and transmission routes, including vertical transmission, were identified.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the need for enhanced surveillance and diagnostic approaches for HPAI H5N1 in marine mammals to inform conservation strategies and assess zoonotic risk. It underscores the importance of monitoring neurological and reproductive system involvement in affected species.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size (n=4) and lack of control animals limit generalizability and the strength of causal inferences. The study relies on post-mortem examinations, which may not capture early or subclinical infections, and the findings are specific to a single outbreak location.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

PA 中的 D347G 对于 H9N2 禽流感 A 病毒对小鼠的致病性至关重要。

D347G in PA is critical for the pathogenicity of H9N2 avian influenza A virus in mice.
作者： Zhehong Zhao, Ye Tian, Wenjie Jiang, Xuefeng Yin, Xinran Chu, Quan Xie, Tuofan Li, Hongxia Shao, Aijian Qin, Jianqiang Ye
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 A 病毒 (IAV) 的某些亚型与人类感染有关(例如 H3N8、H5Ny、H7Ny、H9N2 和 H10Ny),这些病毒引起人畜共患感染的能力进一步增加了禽类 IAV 的公共卫生风险。其中,H9N2 病毒是特别重要的病毒之一,无论是其本身还是作为其他新兴人畜共患禽类 IAV 的内部基因片段的贡献者。近年来,越来越多的 H9N2 病毒株被发现对未经事先适应的小鼠具有高致病性。在这项研究中,我们发现天然存在的 H9N2 分离株 A/chicken/Jiangxi/198/2019 (JX198) 对小鼠具有致命性。为了研究 JX198 对小鼠高毒力的分子基础,产生并测试了一系列重配体和突变体。我们发现PA,尤其是PA中的D347G 突变,是JX198在小鼠中致病性增加的原因。值得注意的是,JX198 PA 中的 D347G 显着改变聚合酶活性、vRNA 产生和噬斑形成。因此,我们的数据表明,PA 中的一种新分子标记 D347G 决定了 H9N2 对小鼠的毒力,PA 中 D347G 的 H9N2 对公共卫生构成了潜在风险,并强调了持续对 H9N2 野毒株进行 监测 的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查自然存在的 H9N2 病毒分离株对小鼠具有高毒力的分子基础,因为一些 禽流感 A 病毒亚型与人类感染和人畜共患 传播 相关,从而增加了公共卫生风险。
设计与对象
该研究涉及生成和测试 H9N2 病毒的一系列重配体和突变体,以确定导致其对小鼠具有高致病性的分子因素。该研究是使用带有 H9N2 分离株 A/chicken/Jiangxi/198/2019 (JX198) 的小鼠模型进行的。
核心方法
为了探索毒力机制,研究人员创建了 JX198 的重配体和突变体,并评估了它们对小鼠聚合酶活性、vRNA 产生和噬菌斑形成的影响。
主要结果
研究发现PA基因,特别是D347G 突变,是JX198在小鼠中致病性增加的原因。D347G 突变 显着改变聚合酶活性、vRNA 产生和噬菌斑形成。
解释与创新
作者解释了他们的发现,表明 H9N2 病毒 PA 基因中的 D347G 突变 是一种新的分子标记,可确定对小鼠的毒力,表明存在潜在的公共健康风险。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了 H9N2 野毒株持续 监测 的重要性,因为 PA 中的 D347G 突变 可能有助于增加毒力和人畜共患潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,并未考虑人类感染动态,但由于直接的实验操作和毒力因子的明确结果,证据是强有力的。

D347G in PA is critical for the pathogenicity of H9N2 avian influenza A virus in mice.

Authors: Zhehong Zhao, Ye Tian, Wenjie Jiang, Xuefeng Yin, Xinran Chu, Quan Xie, Tuofan Li, Hongxia Shao, Aijian Qin, Jianqiang Ye
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Some subtypes of avian influenza A viruses (IAVs) have been associated with human infections (e.g. H3N8, H5Ny, H7Ny, H9N2, and H10Ny), and the ability of these viruses to cause zoonotic infections further increases the public health risk of avian IAVs. Among them, H9N2 virus is one of particular importance, both in its own right and as a contributor of internal gene segments to other emerging zoonotic avian IAVs. In recent years, an increasing number of H9N2 strains have been observed to be highly pathogenic in mice without prior adaptation. In this study, we found that a naturally occurring H9N2 isolate, A/chicken/Jiangxi/198/2019 (JX198), is lethal in mice. To investigate the molecular basis for the high virulence of JX198 in mice, a series of reassortants and mutants were generated and tested. We found that the PA, especially D347G mutation in PA, is responsible for the increased pathogenicity of JX198 in mice. Notably, D347G in PA of JX198 significantly alters the polymerase activity, vRNA production, and plaque-formation. Thus, our data demonstrate that a novel molecular marker, D347G in PA, determines the virulence of H9N2 in mice, posing a potential risk of H9N2 with D347G in PA for public health and highlighting the significance of continuing surveillance of H9N2 field strains.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the molecular basis for the high virulence of a naturally occurring H9N2 isolate in mice, as some avian influenza A virus subtypes have been associated with human infections and zoonotic transmission, increasing public health risks.
Design and population
The study involved generating and testing a series of reassortants and mutants of the H9N2 virus to determine the molecular factors contributing to its high pathogenicity in mice. The research was conducted using a mouse model with the H9N2 isolate A/chicken/Jiangxi/198/2019 (JX198).
Core methods
To explore the virulence mechanisms, the researchers created reassortants and mutants of JX198 and evaluated their effects on polymerase activity, vRNA production, and plaque formation in mice.
Main results
The study found that the PA gene, particularly the D347G mutation, is responsible for the increased pathogenicity of JX198 in mice. The D347G mutation significantly altered polymerase activity, vRNA production, and plaque formation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings to show that the D347G mutation in the PA gene of H9N2 viruses is a novel molecular marker that determines virulence in mice, indicating a potential public health risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of continued surveillance of H9N2 field strains, as the D347G mutation in PA may contribute to increased virulence and zoonotic potential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mouse model and does not account for human infection dynamics, but the evidence is strong due to the direct experimental manipulation and clear results on virulence factors.

亚类转换增强非中和性 H7N9 禽流感 抗体的保护功效。

Subclass switching enhances protective efficacy of a non-neutralizing H7N9 avian influenza antibody.
作者： Yunlong Dou, Jiangyan Zhao, Xiaozheng He, Seung-un Song, Erdene-Ochir Tseren-Ochir, Chang-Seon Song, Xiufan Liu, Jiao Hu, Zenglei Hu
摘要中文翻译非中和抗体通过 Fc 效应子功能有助于抵御 H7N9 亚型 禽流感 病毒。抗体亚类在确定与 Fc 受体 (FcR) 的结合亲和力和下游 Fc 效应子功能方面发挥着关键作用。在本研究中,为了评估抗体亚型转换是否可以增强抗体的保护功效,将针对H7N9病毒血凝素的非中和性单克隆抗体(mAb)亚型从IgG1转换为IgG2a,并评估其活性和保护功效。亚类转换不会导致抗体的抗原结合、血凝抑制和病毒中和活​​性发生显着变化。杂交瘤衍生的 mAb 不能提供针对 H7N9 病毒感染的保护作用。有趣的是,CHO 细胞中产生的 IgG2a 和 IgG1 抗体都提供了针对亚致死 H7N9 病毒攻击引起的 死亡率 的全面保护。在致死攻击后,IgG2a 抗体提供 70% 的保护,而其 IgG1 抗体仅提供 30% 的保护。然而,这两种抗体都没有减少小鼠肺部的病毒载量。此外,与 IgG1 抗体相比,亚类转换 IgG2a 抗体对 FcR 的亲和力显着增加,如该抗体与重组鼠 FcγRI 和鼠巨噬细胞上 FcR 的结合增强所证明。我们的研究结果强调了亚类工程对抗体修饰和治疗性抗体开发的好处。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了针对 H7N9 禽流感 病毒的非中和性单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的亚类转换是否可以通过 Fc 效应子功能增强其保护功效,因为抗体亚类会影响 Fc 受体结合和下游 免疫应答。
设计与对象
该研究在 H7N9 病毒攻击的小鼠模型中使用杂交瘤衍生的和 CHO 细胞产生的抗体,评估了非中和性单克隆抗体在将其亚类从 IgG1 切换为 IgG2a 后针对 H7N9 血凝素的活性和保护功效。
核心方法
针对 H7N9 血凝素的非中和性单克隆抗体亚类从 IgG1 切换为 IgG2a,并在小鼠攻击模型中评估抗原结合、血凝抑制、病毒中和活​​性、Fc 受体结合亲和力和保护功效。
主要结果
亚类转换不会改变抗原结合、血凝抑制或病毒中和活​​性,但与 IgG1 相比,IgG2a 对 FcγRI 和鼠巨噬细胞 FcR 的亲和力显着更高。在亚致死攻击中,IgG1 和 IgG2a 均提供全面保护,而在致死攻击中,IgG2a 提供 70% 的保护,而 IgG1 则为 30%。这两种抗体都不能减少小鼠肺部的病毒载量。
解释与创新
作者解释说,亚类转换通过改善 Fc 受体结合而不改变抗原结合或中和活性来增强保护功效,突出了针对 H7N9 的治疗性抗体工程的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,治疗性抗体的子类工程可以通过增强 Fc 介导的保护来改善 H7N9 感染的临床结果,为未来的 疫苗 和治疗开发策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,没有评估病毒载量减少或长期保护,这限制了其对人类感染的普遍适用性;由于缺乏人类数据和机制深度,证据强度中等。

Subclass switching enhances protective efficacy of a non-neutralizing H7N9 avian influenza antibody.

Authors: Yunlong Dou, Jiangyan Zhao, Xiaozheng He, Seung-un Song, Erdene-Ochir Tseren-Ochir, Chang-Seon Song, Xiufan Liu, Jiao Hu, Zenglei Hu
Original abstract (English)
Non-neutralizing antibodies contribute to protection against the H7N9 subtype avian influenza virus through Fc effector functions. Antibody subclass plays a critical role in determining binding affinity to Fc receptors (FcR) and downstream Fc effector functions. In this study, to assess whether antibody subclass switching can enhance antibody protective efficacy, subclass of a non-neutralizing monoclonal antibody (mAb) against the hemagglutinin of H7N9 virus was switched from IgG1 to IgG2a, and their activity and protective efficacy were assessed. Subclass switching caused no significant changes in antigen binding, hemagglutination-inhibition and virus neutralizing activities of the antibody. The hybridoma-derived mAb conferred no protection against H7N9 virus infection. Interestingly, both IgG2a and IgG1 antibodies produced in CHO cells provided full protection against mortality caused by a sublethal H7N9 virus challenge. Upon lethal challenge, IgG2a antibody conferred 70% protection, whereas its IgG1 counterpart only provided 30% protection. However, both antibodies did not decrease virus loads in mouse lungs. Moreover, compared to the IgG1 antibody, affinity of the subclass-switched IgG2a antibody to FcR was significantly increased, as evidenced by enhanced binding of the antibody to recombinant murine FcγRI and to FcRs on murine macrophages. Our findings highlighted a benefit of subclass-engineering to antibody modification and development of therapeutic antibodies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether subclass switching of a non-neutralizing monoclonal antibody (mAb) against H7N9 avian influenza virus could enhance its protective efficacy through Fc effector functions, given that antibody subclass influences Fc receptor binding and downstream immune responses.
Design and population
The study assessed the activity and protective efficacy of a non-neutralizing mAb against H7N9 hemagglutinin after switching its subclass from IgG1 to IgG2a, using hybridoma-derived and CHO cell-produced antibodies in mouse models challenged with H7N9 virus.
Core methods
The subclass of a non-neutralizing mAb against H7N9 hemagglutinin was switched from IgG1 to IgG2a, and antigen binding, hemagglutination-inhibition, virus neutralizing activities, Fc receptor binding affinity, and protective efficacy were evaluated in mouse challenge models.
Main results
Subclass switching did not alter antigen binding, hemagglutination-inhibition, or virus neutralizing activities, but IgG2a showed significantly higher affinity to FcγRI and murine macrophage FcRs compared to IgG1. In sublethal challenge, both IgG1 and IgG2a provided full protection, whereas in lethal challenge, IgG2a conferred 70% protection versus 30% for IgG1. Neither antibody reduced virus loads in mouse lungs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that subclass switching enhanced protective efficacy by improving Fc receptor engagement without altering antigen-binding or neutralizing activity, highlighting a novel strategy for therapeutic antibody engineering against H7N9.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that subclass engineering of therapeutic antibodies could improve clinical outcomes for H7N9 infections by enhancing Fc-mediated protection, informing future vaccine and therapeutic development strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to mouse models and did not evaluate virus load reduction or long-term protection, which constrains generalizability to human infections; the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human data and mechanistic depth.

针对不同人畜共患 H5N1 进化枝的一次性多价减毒候选流感 疫苗

A One-Shot Multivalent Live-Attenuated Candidate Influenza Vaccine against Divergent Zoonotic H5N1 Clades
作者： Ahmed M. Elsayed, Ramya S. Barre, Arash Rahmani, Ruby A. Escobedo, Trushar Jeevan, Himadri Nath, Esteban M. Castro, Vinay Shivanna, Aitor Nogales, James J. Kobie
摘要中文翻译摘要 具有人畜共患潜力的遗传多样性高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒的持续出现凸显了开发能够针对多个循环分支提供广泛保护的 疫苗 的迫切需要。在此,我们基于温度敏感 (ts)、冷适应 (ca) 和减毒 (att) 甲型/安娜堡/6/1960 主供体病毒 (MDV) 开发了一种一次性、多价、减毒活流感 疫苗 (LAIV),该病毒融合了来自代表性进化枝的血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 糖蛋白2.3.4.4b (A/Louisiana/12/2024)、2.3.2.1a (A/Victoria/149/2024) 和 2.3.2.1e (A/Cambodia/2302009/2023) H5N1 病毒。用多价 LAIV 对 C57BL/6 小鼠进行单次鼻内 (IN) 免疫,引发了强大的体液 免疫应答,免疫血清对 疫苗 中包含的所有三个 H5N1 进化枝的抗原表现出广泛的交叉反应性。经过同源病毒攻击后,接种疫苗的 C57BL/6 小鼠完全免受疾病侵害,证明了多价 LAIV 的免疫原性和保护功效。通过同时针对具有大流行潜力的抗原不同的 H5N1 谱系,该策略扩大了单个 LAIV 内的抗原覆盖范围,以确认泛 H5N1 保护。总之,这些发现支持这种多价 LAIV 的开发和实施,作为一种广泛保护性的泛 H5N1 LAIV,用于大流行防范。意义 人畜共患病 H5N1 病毒遗传多样性的增加使 疫苗 设计变得复杂。我们基于温度敏感、冷适应和减毒(ts、ca、att)主供体病毒(MDV)流感 A/Ann Arbor/6/1960 主链开发了多价减毒活流感 疫苗 (LAIV),该主链表达 H5N1 进化枝的血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 糖蛋白2.3.4.4b、2.3.2.1a 和 2.3.2.1e。使用多价 LAIV 进行单次鼻内 (IN) 免疫可诱导广泛的交叉反应性中和抗体 (NAb) 反应,并保护实验性疫苗接种动物免受同源致死病毒攻击,这证明多价 LAIV 可以预防人类最关心的 H5N1 病毒分支。这些发现证明了开发和实施这种多价 LAIV 作为人类使用的针对不同 H5N1 病毒的广泛保护性泛 H5N1 LAIV 的可行性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究满足了公共卫生对 疫苗 的迫切需求,该药物可以预防基因多样化的人畜共患 H5N1 流感病毒,这些病毒继续在多个分支中出现并构成大流行风险。
设计与对象
作者使用表达 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b、2.3.2.1a 和 2.3.2.1e 的 HA 和 NA 的 ts/ca/att A/Ann Arbor/6/1960 主干开发了多价减毒活流感 疫苗 (LAIV),并在 C57BL/6 小鼠中进行了评估。
核心方法
对小鼠进行单次鼻内剂量的多价 LAIV,并评估免疫血清与进化枝匹配抗原的交叉反应性;在同源致死攻击后评估保护作用。
主要结果
接种疫苗的小鼠表现出强烈的体液反应,对所有三个 H5N1 进化枝具有广泛的交叉反应性,并且在同源攻击后完全免受疾病影响。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了单次多价 LAIV 的可行性,该疫苗可以扩大抗原覆盖范围,覆盖不同抗原的 H5N1 谱系,代表了一种新的泛 H5N1 策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持将这种多价 LAIV 作为一种具有广泛保护性的泛 H5N1 疫苗 候选药物,用于大流行防范。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于小鼠模型,缺乏人类数据,这限制了普遍性,并且需要谨慎解释证据的强度。

A One-Shot Multivalent Live-Attenuated Candidate Influenza Vaccine against Divergent Zoonotic H5N1 Clades

Authors: Ahmed M. Elsayed, Ramya S. Barre, Arash Rahmani, Ruby A. Escobedo, Trushar Jeevan, Himadri Nath, Esteban M. Castro, Vinay Shivanna, Aitor Nogales, James J. Kobie
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The continued emergence of genetically diverse high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 viruses with zoonotic potential highlights the urgent need for developing vaccines capable of providing broad protection against multiple circulating clades. Here, we developed a one-shot, multivalent, live-attenuated influenza vaccine (LAIV) based on the temperature-sensitive (ts), cold-adapted (ca), and attenuated ( att ) influenza A/Ann Arbor/6/1960 master donor virus (MDV) that incorporates the hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) glycoproteins from representative clades 2.3.4.4b (A/Louisiana/12/2024), 2.3.2.1a (A/Victoria/149/2024), and 2.3.2.1e (A/Cambodia/2302009/2023) H5N1 viruses. A single intranasal (IN) immunization of C57BL/6 mice with the multivalent LAIV elicited robust humoral immune responses, with immune sera exhibiting broad cross-reactivity against antigens from all three H5N1 clades included in the vaccine. Following homologous viral challenge, vaccinated C57BL/6 mice were completely protected from disease, demonstrating the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of the multivalent LAIV. By simultaneously targeting antigenically distinct H5N1 lineages with pandemic potential, this strategy expands antigenic coverage within a single LAIV to confirm pan-H5N1 protection. Together, these findings support the development and implementation of this multivalent LAIV as a broadly protective pan-H5N1 LAIV for pandemic preparedness. Significance The increasing genetic diversity of zoonotic H5N1 viruses complicates vaccine design. We developed a multivalent live-attenuated influenza vaccine (LAIV) based on the temperature-sensitive, cold-adapted, and attenuated ( ts , ca , att ) master donor virus (MDV) influenza A/Ann Arbor/6/1960 backbone that expresses the hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) glycoproteins of H5N1 clades 2.3.4.4b, 2.3.2.1a, and 2.3.2.1e. A single intranasal (IN) immunization with the multivalent LAIV induced broadly cross-reactive neutralizing antibody (NAb) responses and protected experimental vaccinated animals against homologous lethal viral challenge, demonstrating the feasibility of the multivalent LAIV to protect against H5N1 clades of highest concern to humans. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of developing and implementing this multivalent LAIV as a broad protective pan-H5N1 LAIV against divergent H5N1 viruses for human use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for vaccines that can protect against genetically diverse zoonotic H5N1 influenza viruses, which continue to emerge in multiple clades and pose pandemic risk.
Design and population
The authors developed a multivalent live-attenuated influenza vaccine (LAIV) using the ts/ca/att A/Ann Arbor/6/1960 backbone expressing HA and NA from H5N1 clades 2.3.4.4b, 2.3.2.1a, and 2.3.2.1e, and evaluated it in C57BL/6 mice.
Core methods
A single intranasal dose of the multivalent LAIV was administered to mice, and immune sera were assessed for cross-reactivity against clade-matched antigens; protection was evaluated after homologous lethal challenge.
Main results
Vaccinated mice showed robust humoral responses with broad cross-reactivity against all three H5N1 clades and were completely protected from disease following homologous challenge.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the feasibility of a single-shot multivalent LAIV that expands antigenic coverage across antigenically distinct H5N1 lineages, representing a novel pan-H5N1 strategy.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support advancing this multivalent LAIV as a broadly protective pan-H5N1 vaccine candidate for pandemic preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to a mouse model and lacks human data, which constrains generalizability and necessitates cautious interpretation of the strength of evidence.

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 继续在家禽中造成大量 死亡率,并在全球范围内造成 野生鸟类 [1,2]。该研究旨在调查高致病性禽流感的季节性是否随时间而变化。
设计与对象
该研究分析了来自多个地区的全球 HPAI 监测 数据,以评估季节性模式。它包括来自不同地理位置的家禽和 野生鸟类 种群的数据。
核心方法
研究人员审查并分析了 HPAI 监测 数据,以确定季节性趋势。他们将历史数据与最近的观察结果进行比较,以确定是否存在季节性变化。

此前,HPAI表现出明显的冬季季节性。

作者认为,观察到的季节性变化可能表明 HPAI 的 流行病学 发生了变化,可能会影响 监测 和控制策略。

Is the seasonality of highly pathogenic avian influenza changing?

Authors: Krishna Prasad Acharya, Sarita Phuyal, Sher Bahadur Pun
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) continues to cause substantial mortality in poultry and wild birds globally [1,2]. Previously, HPAI showed marked winter seasonality. However, recent studie...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) continues to cause substantial mortality in poultry and wild birds globally [1,2]. The study aimed to investigate whether the seasonality of HPAI is changing over time.
Design and population
The study analyzed global HPAI surveillance data from multiple regions to assess seasonal patterns. It included data from poultry and wild bird populations across different geographic locations.
Core methods
The researchers reviewed and analyzed HPAI surveillance data to identify seasonal trends. They compared historical data with recent observations to determine if there was a shift in seasonality.
Main results
Previously, HPAI showed marked winter seasonality.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that the observed changes in seasonality may indicate a shift in the epidemiology of HPAI, potentially affecting surveillance and control strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more effective surveillance and response strategies for HPAI outbreaks, particularly in regions where seasonal patterns are changing.
Limitations and evidence strength

去糖基化 H7N9 VLP 免疫可增强小鼠针对 禽流感 H7N9 变种的跨品系保护性抗体反应

Immunization with deglycosylated H7N9 VLP enhances cross-strain protective antibody responses against avian influenza H7N9 variants in mice
作者： Asmaul Husna, Yu-Chieh Cheng, Chia-Chun Lai, Tsai-Chuan Weng, Min-Han Lin, Min-Shi Lee, Shih-Che Sue, Wang-Chou Sung, Yu‐Chieh Cheng, Shih‐Che Sue
摘要中文翻译简介 快速进化的 禽流感 H7N9 病毒仍然对全球公共卫生构成威胁。迫切需要开发广谱 疫苗,能够引发针对新出现的病毒变种的功能性跨毒株保护性免疫。方法 N-连接糖基化可以保护关键残基免受免疫识别。在这里,基于 A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 病毒的抗原序列构建了原型病毒样颗粒 (VLP)。利用糖苷酶修剪聚糖分子,以生成单糖基化 VLP (VLPmg) 和非糖基化 VLP (VLPng) 衍生物。对这些 VLP 的结构完整性、抗原性和免疫原性进行了系统评估。结果 VLPmg 和 VLPng 均保持与完全糖基化原型 VLP (VLPfg) 一样的血凝活性和抗原性。用去糖基化的 VLP 进行免疫可以在小鼠体内诱导出强大的病毒特异性抗体和平衡的 Th1/Th2 免疫特征,而滴度与 VLPfg 相当。其中,VLPng针对不同的H7N9病毒株诱导了更高的血凝抑制和中和抗体滴度,表明跨病毒株反应性抗体反应得到改善。结论 这项研究表明,流感 H7N9 VLP 的酶促去糖基化可改善小鼠体内广泛保护性抗体反应的诱导。非糖基化的 H7N9 VLP 代表了潜在的 疫苗 候选药物,可对抗抗原不同的 H7N9 病毒变种的威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决快速进化的 禽流感 H7N9 病毒对全球公共卫生造成的威胁,并开发能够针对新出现的变种引发广谱免疫的 疫苗。
设计与对象
基于A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9病毒的抗原序列构建了原型病毒样颗粒(VLP),并在小鼠中评估了这些VLP的结构完整性、抗原性和免疫原性。
核心方法
N-连接糖基化被确定为一种保护关键残基免受免疫识别的机制,并且糖苷酶被用来修剪聚糖分子以产生单糖基化和非糖基化的VLP衍生物。
主要结果
VLPmg 和 VLPng 均保持与完全糖基化原型 VLP (VLPfg) 相似的血凝活性和抗原性,并且用去糖基化 VLP 进行免疫可在小鼠中诱导强大的病毒特异性抗体和平衡的 Th1/Th2 免疫特征。
解释与创新
该研究表明,流感 H7N9 VLP 的酶促去糖基化可增强小鼠体内广泛保护性抗体反应的诱导,这提出了 疫苗 开发的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,非糖基化的 H7N9 VLP 可以作为潜在的 疫苗 候选药物,以对抗抗原不同的 H7N9 病毒变种的威胁,为改善交叉毒株免疫力提供启示。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠,没有报告人体试验,但证据很充分,因为它系统地评估了 VLP 的结构和免疫原性特性。

Immunization with deglycosylated H7N9 VLP enhances cross-strain protective antibody responses against avian influenza H7N9 variants in mice

Authors: Asmaul Husna, Yu-Chieh Cheng, Chia-Chun Lai, Tsai-Chuan Weng, Min-Han Lin, Min-Shi Lee, Shih-Che Sue, Wang-Chou Sung, Yu‐Chieh Cheng, Shih‐Che Sue
Original abstract (English)
Introduction The rapidly evolving avian influenza H7N9 virus remains a threaten to public health globally. There is an urgent need to develop broad-spectrum vaccines capable of eliciting functional cross-strain protective immunity against emerging viral variants. Methods N-linked glycosylation can shield critical residues from immune recognition. Here, a prototype virus-like particle (VLP) was constructed based on the antigenic sequences derived from A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 virus. Glycosidase enzymes were utilized to trim the glycan molecules for generating monoglycosylated VLP (VLPmg) and nonglycosylated VLP (VLPng) derivates. The structural integrity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity of these VLPs were systematically evaluated. Results Both VLPmg and VLPng maintained hemagglutination activity and antigenicity like the fully glycosylated prototype VLP (VLPfg). Immunization with deglycosylated VLPs induced robust virus-specific antibodies and a balanced Th1/Th2 immune profile in mice, whereas the titers were comparable to that of VLPfg. Among them, VLPng induced higher hemagglutination inhibition and neutralizing antibody titers against different H7N9 virus strains, indicating improved cross-strain reactive antibody responses. Conclusion This study demonstrated the enzymatic deglycosylation of influenza H7N9 VLP improves the induction of broadly protective antibody responses in mice. The nonglycosylated H7N9 VLP represents a potential vaccine candidate to against the threat of antigenically divergent H7N9 virus variants.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the threat posed by the rapidly evolving avian influenza H7N9 virus to global public health and to develop vaccines capable of eliciting broad-spectrum immunity against emerging variants.
Design and population
A prototype virus-like particle (VLP) was constructed based on the antigenic sequences of the A/Taiwan/1/2013 H7N9 virus, and the structural integrity, antigenicity, and immunogenicity of these VLPs were evaluated in mice.
Core methods
N-linked glycosylation was identified as a mechanism that shields critical residues from immune recognition, and glycosidase enzymes were used to trim glycan molecules to generate monoglycosylated and nonglycosylated VLP derivatives.
Main results
Both VLPmg and VLPng maintained hemagglutination activity and antigenicity similar to the fully glycosylated prototype VLP (VLPfg), and immunization with deglycosylated VLPs induced robust virus-specific antibodies and a balanced Th1/Th2 immune profile in mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that enzymatic deglycosylation of influenza H7N9 VLPs enhances the induction of broadly protective antibody responses in mice, suggesting a novel approach to vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nonglycosylated H7N9 VLPs could serve as a potential vaccine candidate to combat the threat of antigenically divergent H7N9 virus variants, offering implications for improved cross-strain immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mice and does not report on human trials, but the evidence is strong as it systematically evaluates the structural and immunogenic properties of the VLPs.

在季节性甲型流感病毒感染患者中,针对 A/H5N1 衍生血凝素肽的 CD4 + T 细胞反应增加。

CD4 + T-cell responses directed towards A/H5N1-derived haemagglutinin peptides increase in patients with seasonal influenza A virus infection.
作者： Lilith F Allen, Louise C Rowntree, Mitchell Jenzen, Ruth R. Hagen, Nathan P Croft, Fiona James, Genevieve E. Martin, Anthony W. Purcell, Patricia T Illing, Steven Y. C. Tong
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 最近全球范围内鸟类和哺乳动物中发生的 A/H5N1 流感病毒感染事件再次引发人们对在全球普遍免疫学上未接受过免疫的人群中出现的新出现的 H5N1 相关流感大流行的担忧。已在人类体内发现了针对内部和表面流感蛋白的 A/H1N1 和 A/H5N1 亚型之间的交叉反应性 CD4+ T 细胞,这表明对严重和致命的 A/H5N1 感染有一定的保护作用。方法 为了检测针对 H1 和 H5 病毒的 T 细胞反应,我们刺激了因季节性甲型流感病毒住院的患者以及健康个体的 PBMC,其中包含 A/Vietnam/1203/2004 (H5N1) 和 A/New York/18/2009(H1N1-pdm09-like)病毒的血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 重叠肽库。结果 大多数急性感染者和健康参与者(分别为 100% 和 75%)对 H1-HA 和 H5-HA 具有可检测到的 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应,而 30-80% 的个体具有可检测到的 H5-NA 反应。H1 和 H5 HA 特异性 CD4+ T 细胞反应高度相关,表明针对 H1 和 H5 亚型的 T 细胞反应之间存在一定程度的交叉反应。与健康参与者相比,流感患者的 HA 特异性 CD4+ T 细胞反应增强。我们发现,无论感染状态如何,1968 年之前和之后出生的人的 H1 和 H5 特异性 T 细胞反应没有差异。H1 和 H5 病毒的序列比对发现 HA2 茎亚基具有高度保守性 (81.53%)。CD4+ T 细胞对免疫显性 HA306-318 表位的免疫,受到几个 HLA-DRB1 分子的限制,揭示了第 1 组和第 2 组 HA 亚型之间有限的交叉反应性。结论 我们定义了季节性甲型流感病毒感染期间相关的 H1 和 H5 特异性 CD4+ T 细胞反应,可能反映了一定程度的 H1/H5 T 细胞交叉反应性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了人类季节性流感 A/H1N1 和禽类 A/H5N1 病毒之间是否存在交叉反应性 CD4+ T 细胞反应,其动机是全球对最近鸟类和哺乳动物 暴发 后免疫学幼稚人群中潜在的 H5N1 大流行的担忧。
设计与对象
该研究招募了因季节性甲型流感病毒感染住院的患者和健康个体,收集外周血单核细胞 (PBMC),以评估 T 细胞针对 A/H5N1 和 A/H1N1 病毒的血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 肽的反应。
核心方法
使用跨越 A/Vietnam/1203/2004 (H5N1) 和 A/New York/18/2009 (H1N1-pdm09-like) 病毒的 HA 和 NA 蛋白的重叠肽池刺激外周血单核细胞 (PBMC),以使用标准免疫测定法检测 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应。
主要结果
所有急性感染参与者和 75% 的健康参与者对 H1-HA 和 H5-HA 都有可检测到的 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应,而 30-80% 则表现出可检测到的 H5-NA 反应;流感患者的 HA 特异性 CD4+ T 细胞反应高于健康参与者。
解释与创新
作者将 H1-和 H5-HA 特异性 CD4+ T 细胞反应之间的强相关性解释为交叉反应性的证据,并指出 HA2 茎亚基 (81.53%) 的高度保守性和跨 HA 亚型的免疫显性 HA306-318 表位的有限交叉反应性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对季节性流感的现有 CD4+ T 细胞免疫可能对严重 H5N1 感染提供部分保护,为未来针对保守 HA 区域的 疫苗 策略提供信息,以扩大流感免疫力。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小、缺乏直接H5N1感染病例以及依赖体外肽刺激测定的限制,这可能无法完全反映体内免疫应答,需要谨慎地将证据强度解释为中等。

CD4 + T-cell responses directed towards A/H5N1-derived haemagglutinin peptides increase in patients with seasonal influenza A virus infection.

Authors: Lilith F Allen, Louise C Rowntree, Mitchell Jenzen, Ruth R. Hagen, Nathan P Croft, Fiona James, Genevieve E. Martin, Anthony W. Purcell, Patricia T Illing, Steven Y. C. Tong
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objectives Recent outbreak of influenza A/H5N1 viral infections in birds and mammals worldwide prompted renewed concerns over an emerging H5N1‐related influenza pandemic in the generally immunologically‐naive global population. Cross‐reactive CD4+ T cells between A/H1N1 and A/H5N1 subtypes targeting internal and surface influenza proteins have been identified in humans, indicating some protection against severe and fatal A/H5N1 infection. Methods To detect T‐cell responses towards H1 and H5 viruses, we stimulated PBMCs from patients hospitalised with seasonal influenza A viruses, alongside healthy individuals, with overlapping peptide pools spanning hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) of A/Vietnam/1203/2004 (H5N1) and A/New York/18/2009 (H1N1‐pdm09‐like) viruses. Results Majority of acutely infected and healthy participants (100% and 75%, respectively) had detectable CD4+ and CD8+ T‐cell responses towards H1‐HA and H5‐HA, whereas 30–80% of individuals had detectable H5‐NA responses. H1 and H5 HA‐specific CD4+ T‐cell responses highly correlated, suggesting some level of cross‐reactivity between T‐cell responses directed against H1 and H5 subtypes. HA‐specific CD4+ T‐cell responses were increased in influenza patients compared to healthy participants. We found no differences in H1‐ and H5‐specific T cell responses between those born pre‐ and post‐1968, irrespective of infection status. Sequence alignment of H1 and H5 viruses identified high conservation in the HA2 stalk subunit (81.53%). CD4+ T‐cell immunity towards immunodominant HA306–318 epitope, restricted by several HLA‐DRB1 molecules, revealed limited cross‐reactivity between Group 1 and Group 2 HA‐subtypes. Conclusion We define correlative H1‐ and H5‐specific CD4+ T‐cell responses during seasonal influenza A virus infection, likely reflecting some level of H1/H5 T cell cross‐reactivity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether cross-reactive CD4+ T-cell responses exist between seasonal influenza A/H1N1 and avian A/H5N1 viruses in humans, motivated by global concerns over potential H5N1 pandemics in immunologically naive populations following recent outbreaks in birds and mammals.
Design and population
The study enrolled patients hospitalized with seasonal influenza A virus infection and healthy individuals, collecting peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) to assess T-cell responses against hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) peptides from A/H5N1 and A/H1N1 viruses.
Core methods
Peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) were stimulated with overlapping peptide pools spanning HA and NA proteins from A/Vietnam/1203/2004 (H5N1) and A/New York/18/2009 (H1N1-pdm09-like) viruses to detect CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses using standard immunological assays.
Main results
All acutely infected participants and 75% of healthy participants had detectable CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses to H1-HA and H5-HA, while 30–80% showed detectable H5-NA responses; HA-specific CD4+ T-cell responses were higher in influenza patients than in healthy participants.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the strong correlation between H1- and H5-HA-specific CD4+ T-cell responses as evidence of cross-reactivity, noting high conservation in the HA2 stalk subunit (81.53%) and limited cross-reactivity at the immunodominant HA306–318 epitope across HA subtypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that pre-existing CD4+ T-cell immunity from seasonal influenza may provide partial protection against severe H5N1 infection, informing future vaccine strategies targeting conserved HA regions to broaden influenza immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size, lack of direct H5N1 infection cases, and reliance on in vitro peptide stimulation assays, which may not fully reflect in vivo immune responses, warranting cautious interpretation of the evidence strength as moderate.

欧洲 H5N1 禽流感 暴发 的时空建模(2021-2024)

Spatio-temporal Modelling of H5N1 Avian Influenza Outbreaks in Europe (2021-2024)
作者： Mehak Jindal, Samsung Lim, C. Raina MacIntyre
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI),特别是 H5N1 毒株,对整个欧洲的动物健康、生物多样性和粮食安全构成重大持续威胁。了解 禽流感 风险何时何地加剧对于有针对性的 监测 和快速响应至关重要。本研究开发了一个数据驱动的时空框架,整合了地理空间、生态和气候数据集,以解释和预测 2021 年至 2024 年间 H5N1 暴发 的动态。使用负二项分布假设的分层地方流行病模型分析了每周国家级 暴发 计数(208 周,37 个国家)。环境协变量、鸟类物种密度和人口指标被纳入地方性和自回归部分。使用滚动一步预测来评估模型性能,该预测通过适当的评分规则(对数得分和排名概率得分)和校准诊断进行评估。所提出的框架大大优于仅回归的负二项式基线,将平均 logS 降低了约 29%,将 RPS 降低了 49%,同时表现出改进的概率校准。结果表明,H5N1 传播 是由生态驱动因素和当地持久性机制构成的,而不是纯粹的季节性影响。雁形目、鸫形目和鹈鹕目密度被确定为导致病毒传播的关键候鸟科。地方病-流行病模型实现了较高的预测准确性,每周观察到的 暴发 计数大部分落在中心预测区间内(RPS = 0.76,logS = 0.61)。总体而言,所提出的框架提供了一种可扩展的方法,用于将生态和空间信息整合到 HPAI 监测 预警系统中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 继续威胁整个欧洲的动物健康、生物多样性和粮食安全,迫切需要了解风险在何时何地加剧,以实现有针对性的 高致病性禽流感 和快速响应。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一个数据驱动的时空框架,整合了地理空间、生态和气候数据集,利用 37 个国家在 208 周内的每周国家级 暴发 计数来解释和预测 2021 年至 2024 年欧洲的 H5N1 暴发。
核心方法
使用具有负二项分布的分层流行病模型对每周国家级 暴发 计数进行分析,将环境协变量、鸟类物种密度和人口指标纳入地方病和自回归成分。
主要结果
地方病-流行病模型的性能优于仅回归的负二项式基线,平均 logS 降低约 29%,RPS 降低 49%,并且概率校准得到改进,预测精度较高(RPS = 0.76,logS = 0.61)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,H5N1 传播 是由生态驱动因素和当地持久性机制构成的,而不是纯粹的季节性影响,他们认为雁形目、鸬鹚目和鹈鹕目密度是病毒传播的关键因素。
科研与公卫意义
所提出的框架提供了一种可扩展的方法,用于将生态和空间信息整合到 HPAI 监测 预警系统中,从而能够更精确地定位 监测 和响应工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于国家层面的汇总数据和模型框架,该框架可能无法捕捉精细尺度的 传播 动态,从而限制了对当地情况的普遍适用性,并将证据强度限制为中等。

Spatio-temporal Modelling of H5N1 Avian Influenza Outbreaks in Europe (2021-2024)

Authors: Mehak Jindal, Samsung Lim, C. Raina MacIntyre
Original abstract (English)
Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI), particularly the H5N1 strain, poses a significant ongoing threat to animal health, biodiversity and food security across Europe. Understanding where and when avian influenza risks intensify is essential for targeted surveillance and rapid response. This study develops a data-driven spatio-temporal framework that integrates geospatial, ecological and climatic datasets to explain and forecast the dynamics of H5N1 outbreaks between 2021 and 2024. Weekly country-level outbreak counts (208 weeks, 37 countries) were analysed using a hierarchical endemic-epidemic model with an assumption of Negative Binomial distribution. Environmental covariates, bird-species densities, and human population metrics were incorporated into endemic and autoregressive components. Model performance was evaluated using rolling one-step-ahead forecasts assessed by proper scoring rules (logarithmic score and ranked probability score) and calibration diagnostics. The proposed framework substantially outperformed a regression-only Negative Binomial baseline, reducing mean logS by approximately 29% and RPS by 49%, while exhibiting improved probabilistic calibration. Results indicate that H5N1 transmission is structured by ecological drivers and local persistence mechanisms rather than purely seasonal effects. Anseriformes, Charadriiformes and Pelecaniformes densities were identified as the key migratory bird families contributing to the viral spread. The endemic-epidemic model achieved high forecast accuracy, with majority of the of observed weekly outbreak counts falling within central predictive intervals (RPS = 0.76, logS = 0.61). Overall, the proposed framework provides a scalable approach for integrating ecological and spatial information into early-warning systems for HPAI surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 continues to threaten animal health, biodiversity and food security across Europe, creating an urgent need to understand where and when risks intensify for targeted surveillance and rapid response.
Design and population
The study developed a data-driven spatio-temporal framework integrating geospatial, ecological and climatic datasets to explain and forecast H5N1 outbreaks in Europe between 2021 and 2024, using weekly country-level outbreak counts from 37 countries over 208 weeks.
Core methods
Weekly country-level outbreak counts were analysed using a hierarchical endemic-epidemic model with a Negative Binomial distribution, incorporating environmental covariates, bird-species densities and human population metrics into endemic and autoregressive components.
Main results
The endemic-epidemic model outperformed a regression-only Negative Binomial baseline by reducing mean logS by approximately 29% and RPS by 49%, with improved probabilistic calibration and high forecast accuracy (RPS = 0.76, logS = 0.61).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that H5N1 transmission is structured by ecological drivers and local persistence mechanisms rather than purely seasonal effects, and they identify Anseriformes, Charadriiformes and Pelecaniformes densities as key contributors to viral spread.
Scientific and public-health significance
The proposed framework provides a scalable approach for integrating ecological and spatial information into early-warning systems for HPAI surveillance, enabling more precise targeting of surveillance and response efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on country-level aggregated data and a model framework that may not capture fine-scale transmission dynamics, limiting generalisability to local contexts and constraining evidence strength to moderate.

不同内部基因主链灭活H7N9 禽流感 疫苗的免疫原性比较

Comparative Immunogenicity of Inactivated H7N9 Avian Influenza Vaccines with Different Internal Gene Backbones
作者： Yi Liu, Mengyuan Bai, Tao Zhang, Yunqi Cui, Xiaowen Du, Lihong Huang, Jiahao Zhang, Ming Liao, Wenbao Qi
摘要中文翻译H7N9 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 对家禽和公共卫生构成持续威胁。尽管疫苗接种在中国广泛传播,但快速的抗原漂移和重配使得疫苗毒株的频繁更新成为必要。对中国各省分离株(2019年至2023年)的系统发育分析表明,虽然表面基因多样化,但内部基因盒仍然保守,但与其他亚型积极重配,表明内部基因兼容性可能影响疫苗的性能。我们选择了 H7N9 毒株 A/鸡/中国东北/19854-6/2019 (E2),其具有多元血凝素 (HA) 裂解位点并预测了双重受体结合亲和力。为了实现 疫苗 的安全开发,我们修改了 HA 裂解位点以生成低致病性菌株 (E2-Δ)。利用反向遗传学,我们构建了三种重组病毒:E2-Δ(保留当代内部基因)、CVI-E2和CVII-E2(分别包含来自商业使用的供体菌株CVI和CVII的内部基因)。在无特定病原体 (SPF) 鸡中评估了灭活的 疫苗。E2-Δ 诱导的 HI 抗体滴度与 CVI-E2 相当,并且显着高于 CVII-E2,并且在探索性分析中针对最近的一些 H7N9 变种表现出略高的交叉反应 HI 滴度。所有 疫苗 均提供完整的同源保护,减少病毒脱落,并且在挑战试验中没有临床症状。我们的研究结果表明,内部基因主链兼容性可能会影响 疫苗 的免疫原性。虽然 E2-Δ 的性能优于 CVII-E2,但与 CVI-E2 相当,表明某些传统骨架可能仍然适合 H7N9 疫苗 的开发。这种方法需要进一步验证,以支持 H7N9 和潜在其他 禽流感 亚型的改进 疫苗 设计策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管开展了 疫苗接种 活动,H7N9 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 仍然是公共卫生威胁和家禽挑战,其抗原漂移和重配推动了对更新 疫苗 毒株的需求。
设计与对象
该研究构建了三种内部基因骨架不同的灭活 H7N9 疫苗,并评估了无特定病原体 (SPF) 鸡的免疫原性和保护作用。
核心方法
反向遗传学产生了 E2-Δ(低致病性 HA 裂解位点变体)和两个具有当代或供体菌株内部基因的重组体(CVI-E2 和 CVII-E2);制备并测试了灭活的疫苗。
主要结果
E2-Δ 诱导的血凝抑制 (HI) 滴度与 CVI-E2 相当,高于 CVII-E2,并且针对一些最近的 H7N9 变种的交叉反应 HI 滴度略高;所有 疫苗 均提供完全同源保护并减少病毒脱落,且无临床症状。
解释与创新
作者解释说,内部基因主链兼容性影响 疫苗 免疫原性,指出 E2-Δ 优于 CVII-E2,但与 CVI-E2 相当,并提出了这种改进 H7N9 疫苗 设计的策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,选择与循环表面抗原兼容的内部基因主链可能会改善 H7N9 疫苗 性能并支持更广泛的 禽流感 疫苗 策略。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于 SPF 鸡的摘要报告,缺乏人类数据,并且没有量化交叉保护或统计显着性,需要谨慎解释和进一步验证。

Comparative Immunogenicity of Inactivated H7N9 Avian Influenza Vaccines with Different Internal Gene Backbones

Authors: Yi Liu, Mengyuan Bai, Tao Zhang, Yunqi Cui, Xiaowen Du, Lihong Huang, Jiahao Zhang, Ming Liao, Wenbao Qi
Original abstract (English)
H7N9 avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a persistent threat to poultry and public health. Despite widespread vaccination in China, rapid antigenic drift and reassortment necessitate frequent updates of vaccine strains. Phylogenetic analysis of isolates from Chinese provinces (from 2019 to 2023) showed that while surface genes diversified, the internal gene cassette remained conserved yet actively reassorted with other subtypes, suggesting internal gene compatibility may influence vaccine performance. We selected the H7N9 strain A/chicken/Northeast China/19854-6/2019 (E2), which harbors a polybasic Hemagglutinin (HA) cleavage site and predicted dual receptor-binding affinity. To enable safe vaccine development, we modified the HA cleavage site to generate a low-pathogenicity strain (E2-Δ). Using reverse genetics, we constructed three recombinant viruses: E2-Δ (retaining contemporary internal genes), CVI-E2, and CVII-E2 (containing internal genes from commercially used donor strains CVI and CVII, respectively). Inactivated vaccines were evaluated in specific-pathogen-free (SPF) chickens. E2-Δ induced HI antibody titers comparable to those of CVI-E2 and significantly higher than those of CVII-E2, and showed modestly higher cross-reactive HI titers against some recent H7N9 variants in exploratory analyses. All vaccines provided complete homologous protection with reduced viral shedding and no clinical signs in challenge trials. Our findings suggest that internal gene backbone compatibility may influence vaccine immunogenicity. While E2-Δ outperformed CVII-E2, it was comparable to CVI-E2, indicating that certain traditional backbones may still be suitable for H7N9 vaccine development. This approach warrants further validation to support a refined vaccine design strategy for H7N9 and potentially other avian influenza subtypes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
H7N9 avian influenza virus (AIV) remains a public-health threat and poultry challenge despite vaccination campaigns, with antigenic drift and reassortment driving the need for updated vaccine strains.
Design and population
The study constructed three inactivated H7N9 vaccines differing by internal gene backbones and evaluated immunogenicity and protection in specific-pathogen-free (SPF) chickens.
Core methods
Reverse genetics generated E2-Δ (low-pathogenicity HA cleavage site variant) and two recombinants (CVI-E2 and CVII-E2) with contemporary or donor-strain internal genes; inactivated vaccines were prepared and tested.
Main results
E2-Δ induced hemagglutination-inhibition (HI) titers comparable to CVI-E2 and higher than CVII-E2, with modestly higher cross-reactive HI titers against some recent H7N9 variants; all vaccines provided complete homologous protection and reduced viral shedding without clinical signs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that internal gene backbone compatibility influences vaccine immunogenicity, noting E2-Δ’s superiority over CVII-E2 yet parity with CVI-E2, and propose this strategy for refining H7N9 vaccine design.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that selecting internal gene backbones compatible with circulating surface antigens may improve H7N9 vaccine performance and support broader avian influenza vaccine strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to an abstract-only report in SPF chickens, lacks human data, and does not quantify cross-protection or statistical significance, warranting cautious interpretation and further validation.

2024-2025年季节从日本家禽和野生鸟类中分离出的高致病性禽流感 A (H5N1)病毒

High pathogenicity avian influenza A (H5N1) viruses isolated from poultry and wild birds in Japan during the 2024–2025 season
作者： Yoshihiro Takadate, Hayate Nishiura, Asuka Kumagai, Junki Mine, Ryota Tsunekuni, Saki Sakuma, Kosuke Soda, Mana Esaki, Kosuke Okuya, Kei Nabeshima
摘要中文翻译摘要 分支 2.3.4.4b 的 H5N1 高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒继续在 野生鸟类 和家禽中在全球范围内传播,并通过基因重配实现多样化。自2024年以来,美国奶牛中报告了H5N1病毒的跨物种溢出。鉴于 2024-2025 年季节期间,家禽中存在大量 HPAI 暴发 和日本的 野生鸟类,我们旨在检查这些病毒的遗传多样性、致病性和 宿主 范围。系统发育分析显示,2024-2025年季节的所有病毒均属于进化枝2.3.4.4b中的G2d和G2c亚群,并分为11个基因型(G2d:6个基因型,G2c:5个基因型)。其中,G2d-0 是家禽和 野生鸟类 中的优势基因型。通过 G2d 和 G2c 病毒之间的重配出现了新的基因型。鸡的实验感染表明,基因型之间的致病性相当,尽管接种 G2d-0 和 G2c-13 病毒的鸡的病毒脱落量较高(这两种基因型在 2024-2025 年季节的多个 暴发 中得到证实),表明 传播 具有较高的潜力。体外测定显示,与美国奶牛 暴发 的 B3.13 基因型相比,日本分离株对牛乳腺上皮细胞的感染性明显较低,表明牛感染的风险有限。总之,这些发现强调了 HPAI 病毒在 G2d 和 G2c 亚群中持续的遗传多样性,并强调了持续对这些病毒进行体内和体外表征以监测基因型、致病性和 跨物种溢出 对牛的潜在风险的变化的重要性。重要性 H5N1 高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒已在全球范围内的家禽和 野生鸟类 中传播。自 2024 年以来,H5N1 病毒也在美国的奶牛中传播。日本在 2024-2025 年季节报告了许多 HPAI 病例。在此,使用从家禽中分离的菌株和 野生鸟类 进行系统发育分析,可以对其遗传背景进行详细调查,这对于阐明引入途径和 进化 至关重要。致病性调查可能有助于预测 暴发 频率。这项研究表明,多种基因型的HPAI病毒已被引入日本,感染连续暴发基因型的鸡表现出较高的病毒排泄率。此外,比较牛细胞的感染性揭示了牛感染的风险。日本尚未报告牛感染 H5N1 病毒,我们的结果表明风险仍然较低。这些综合分析为制定针对 HPAI 暴发 的有效策略提供了重要见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了分支 2.3.4.4b 的 H5N1 高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒在 野生鸟类 和家禽中的持续全球传播和多样化,以及自 2024 年以来在美国奶牛中最近出现的 跨物种溢出。
设计与对象
该研究检查了 2024-2025 年季节从日本家禽和 野生鸟类 中分离出的 HPAI 病毒,重点关注遗传多样性、致病性和 宿主 范围。
核心方法
进行系统发育分析以确定遗传背景和亚组分类,并通过鸡的实验感染评估致病性和病毒脱落。体外测定评估了牛乳腺上皮细胞的感染性。
主要结果
所有病毒均属于进化枝2.3.4.4b中的G2d和G2c亚组,分为11个基因型(6个G2d和5个G2c)。G2d-0 是家禽和 野生鸟类 中的显性基因型。接种 G2d-0 和 G2c-13 的鸡表现出较高的病毒脱落率,表明 传播 潜力较高。
解释与创新
作者解释说,新的基因型是通过 G2d 和 G2c 病毒之间的重配出现的,并且日本牛感染的风险仍然较低,因为与美国奶牛 暴发 的 B3.13 基因型相比,日本分离株在牛细胞中的感染性明显较低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了日本 HPAI 病毒持续的遗传多样性,并强调需要持续进行 监测 和表征,以监测基因型、致病性和对牛的潜在 跨物种溢出 风险的变化。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于系统发育和实验数据,没有日本牛感染的直接证据,而且仅抽象的证据限制了对方法和结果的详细评估,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

High pathogenicity avian influenza A (H5N1) viruses isolated from poultry and wild birds in Japan during the 2024–2025 season

Authors: Yoshihiro Takadate, Hayate Nishiura, Asuka Kumagai, Junki Mine, Ryota Tsunekuni, Saki Sakuma, Kosuke Soda, Mana Esaki, Kosuke Okuya, Kei Nabeshima
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT H5N1 high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b continue to spread globally among wild birds and poultry, and diversify through gene reassortment. Since 2024, spillovers of H5N1 viruses have been reported in dairy cattle in the United States. Given the numerous HPAI outbreaks in poultry and wild birds in Japan during the 2024–2025 season, we aimed to examine the genetic diversity, pathogenicity, and host range of these viruses. Phylogenetic analyses revealed that all viruses in the 2024–2025 season belonged to G2d and G2c subgroups in clade 2.3.4.4b and were divided into 11 genotypes (G2d: six genotypes, G2c: five genotypes). Of these, G2d-0 was the dominant genotype in both poultry and wild birds. Novel genotypes emerged through reassortment between G2d and G2c viruses. Experimental infection of chickens revealed comparable pathogenicity among genotypes, although the amounts of viral shedding were high in chickens inoculated with G2d-0 and G2c-13 viruses, which were genotypes confirmed in multiple outbreaks in the 2024–2025 season, suggesting high transmission potential. In vitro assays revealed markedly lower infectivity of Japanese isolates in bovine mammary epithelial cells compared with that of the B3.13 genotype responsible for dairy cattle outbreaks in the United States, indicating limited risk of cattle infection. Together, these findings underscore ongoing genetic diversification of HPAI viruses in G2d and G2c subgroups and highlight the importance of continued in vivo and in vitro characterization of these viruses to monitor changes in genotypes, pathogenicity, and potential risk of spillover to cattle. IMPORTANCE H5N1 high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) viruses have spread in poultry and wild birds worldwide. H5N1 viruses have also spread in dairy cattle in the United States since 2024. Japan reported numerous HPAI cases during the 2024–2025 season. Herein, phylogenetic analyses using strains isolated from poultry and wild birds enabled a detailed investigation of their genetic backgrounds, which is essential for elucidating introduction routes and evolution. Investigation of pathogenicity may help predict the outbreak frequency. This study revealed that HPAI viruses of multiple genotypes had been introduced into Japan, and chickens infected with genotypes detected from continuous outbreaks exhibited high virus shedding. In addition, comparing infectivity in bovine cells revealed the risk of cattle infection. H5N1 infection had not been reported in cattle in Japan, and our results suggest that the risk remains low. These integrated analyses provide key insights for developing effective strategies against HPAI outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the ongoing global spread and diversification of H5N1 high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b among wild birds and poultry, alongside recent spillovers in dairy cattle in the United States since 2024.
Design and population
The study examined HPAI viruses isolated from poultry and wild birds in Japan during the 2024–2025 season, focusing on genetic diversity, pathogenicity, and host range.
Core methods
Phylogenetic analyses were performed to determine genetic backgrounds and subgroup classifications, and experimental infection of chickens assessed pathogenicity and viral shedding. In vitro assays evaluated infectivity in bovine mammary epithelial cells.
Main results
All viruses belonged to G2d and G2c subgroups in clade 2.3.4.4b, divided into 11 genotypes (six G2d and five G2c). G2d-0 was the dominant genotype in both poultry and wild birds. Chickens inoculated with G2d-0 and G2c-13 showed high viral shedding, indicating high transmission potential.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that novel genotypes emerged through reassortment between G2d and G2c viruses, and that the risk of cattle infection in Japan remains low due to markedly lower infectivity of Japanese isolates in bovine cells compared with the B3.13 genotype responsible for U.S. dairy cattle outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight ongoing genetic diversification of HPAI viruses in Japan and underscore the need for continued surveillance and characterization to monitor changes in genotypes, pathogenicity, and potential spillover risks to cattle.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on phylogenetic and experimental data without direct evidence of cattle infection in Japan, and the abstract-only evidence restricts detailed assessment of methods and results, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

禽流感 使用机器学习在印度绘制风险地图

Avian influenza risk mapping in India using machine learning
作者： Satish S. Gaikwad, T. R. Arun, Basavaraj Shrinivasa, Pragya Yadav, Babasaheb Tandale
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 仍然对印度的家禽生产和 One-Health 监测 构成经常性威胁。我们使用 2006 年 1 月至 2024 年 4 月精选的 暴发 记录(主要是 HPAI H5N1 和 H5N8)、缓冲伪缺失采样、H3 分辨率 7 六边形以及 94 个环境、牲畜、土地覆盖和人为预测因子,开发了印度国家相对空间风险地图。在 5 倍空间块交叉验证下,训练了 8 个基本分类器,并且所有分类器的表现都高于机会 (AUC > 0.76)。高斯过程堆叠元学习器实现了最高的 AUC(0.853),但相对于最强的个体基础学习器随机森林(AUC 0.851;Brier 0.155;ECE 0.079)的改进很小,并且在统计上不显着。它的主要附加值是一个伴随的不确定性层,即 GPR 后标准差,它显示出基本模型之间的集合分歧的内部一致性(r = 0.74,p < 0.001)。特征归因将人口密度、粗养鸡密度、六月降水量和季节性湿度变量列为与模型输出最相关的预测变量,其解释受到被动 监测 偏差和多重共线性的限制。由此产生的相对空间风险面、预测不确定性面和分区风险不确定性分类层将东部、东北部、沿海和选定的南部地区确定为目标 监测 和前瞻性验证的优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 仍然对印度的家禽生产和 One-Health 监测 构成经常性威胁。
设计与对象

核心方法
在 5 倍空间块交叉验证下,训练了 8 个基本分类器,并且所有分类器的表现都高于机会 (AUC > 0.76)。
主要结果
高斯过程堆叠元学习器实现了最高的 AUC(0.853),但相对于最强的个体基础学习器随机森林(AUC 0.851;Brier 0.155;ECE 0.079)的改进很小,并且在统计上不显着。
解释与创新
它的主要附加值是一个伴随的不确定性层,即 GPR 后标准差,它显示出基本模型之间的集合分歧的内部一致性(r = 0.74,p < 0.001)。
科研与公卫意义
由此产生的相对空间风险面、预测不确定性面和分区风险不确定性分类层将东部、东北部、沿海和选定的南部地区确定为目标 监测 和前瞻性验证的优先事项。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Avian influenza risk mapping in India using machine learning

Authors: Satish S. Gaikwad, T. R. Arun, Basavaraj Shrinivasa, Pragya Yadav, Babasaheb Tandale
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) remains a recurrent threat to poultry production and One-Health surveillance in India. We developed a national relative spatial risk map for India using curated outbreak records spanning January 2006 to April 2024 (predominantly HPAI H5N1 and H5N8), buffered pseudo-absence sampling, H3 resolution-7 hexagons, and 94 environmental, livestock, land-cover, and anthropogenic predictors. Under 5-fold spatial block cross-validation, eight base classifiers were trained and all performed above chance (AUC > 0.76). The Gaussian-process stacked meta-learner achieved the highest AUC (0.853), but the improvement over the strongest individual base learner, Random Forest (AUC 0.851; Brier 0.155; ECE 0.079), was small and statistically non-significant. Its principal added value was a companion uncertainty layer, the GPR posterior standard deviation, which showed internal consistency with ensemble disagreement across base models ( r = 0.74, p < 0.001). Feature attribution ranked human population density, extensive chicken density, June precipitation, and seasonal humidity variables among the predictors most associated with model outputs, with interpretation constrained by passive-surveillance bias and multicollinearity. The resulting relative spatial risk surface, prediction-uncertainty surface, and subdistrict risk-uncertainty classification layers identify eastern, northeastern, coastal, and selected southern regions as priorities for targeted surveillance and prospective validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) remains a recurrent threat to poultry production and One-Health surveillance in India.
Design and population
We developed a national relative spatial risk map for India using curated outbreak records spanning January 2006 to April 2024 (predominantly HPAI H5N1 and H5N8), buffered pseudo-absence sampling, H3 resolution-7 hexagons, and 94 environmental, livestock, land-cover, and anthropogenic predictors.
Core methods
Under 5-fold spatial block cross-validation, eight base classifiers were trained and all performed above chance (AUC > 0.76).
Main results
The Gaussian-process stacked meta-learner achieved the highest AUC (0.853), but the improvement over the strongest individual base learner, Random Forest (AUC 0.851; Brier 0.155; ECE 0.079), was small and statistically non-significant.
Interpretation and novelty
Its principal added value was a companion uncertainty layer, the GPR posterior standard deviation, which showed internal consistency with ensemble disagreement across base models ( r = 0.74, p < 0.001).
Scientific and public-health significance
The resulting relative spatial risk surface, prediction-uncertainty surface, and subdistrict risk-uncertainty classification layers identify eastern, northeastern, coastal, and selected southern regions as priorities for targeted surveillance and prospective validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的牛顿差分优化

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2
作者： Siti Amiroch
摘要中文翻译禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。本研究提出了牛顿除差(NDD)优化,这是一种轻量级正对角曲率感知训练策略,作为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。NDD 在支架密钥和相似性聚类协议下的 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC(接收者操作特征 - 曲线下面积)和 PR-AUC(曲线下精确召回面积),并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。这项工作的新颖性在于首次系统评估 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在稀疏高维配体筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案,尽管在实际 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前仍然需要外部验证和前瞻性实验确认。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。
设计与对象
本研究提出了牛顿除差(NDD)优化,这是一种轻量级正对角曲率感知训练策略,作为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。
核心方法
由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。
主要结果
NDD 在支架关键和相似性聚类协议下在 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC 和 PR-AUC,并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。
解释与创新
架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。
科研与公卫意义
NDD 在基于稀疏高维配体的筛选设置中为 抗病毒药物 针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的优先级提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的 NDD 性能的外部验证或前瞻性实验确认。

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2

Authors: Siti Amiroch
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening. This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization, a lightweight positive diagonal curvature-aware training strategy, as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols. An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds. NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC (Receiver Operating Characteriztic – Area Under the Curve) and PR-AUC (Precision-Recall Area Under the Curve) on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance. The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings, although external validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization.

Interpretation and novelty

The supplied evidence does not report external validation or prospective experimental confirmation of NDD performance against avian influenza A/H9N2.
