禽流感 A(H5) 高通量 RT-qPCR 亚型分型的样本池验证

Validation of sample pooling for high-throughput RT-qPCR subtyping of avian influenza A(H5)
作者： Grant P. Higerd-Rusli, Seth A. Hoffman, Abraar Karan, ChunHong Huang, Malaya K. Sahoo, Ingrid E. A. Morante, Matthew M. Hernandez, Benjamin A. Pinsky
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 因其高历史毒力和不断增加的哺乳动物 宿主 范围而成为全球健康威胁,正如 2024 年至 2025 年美国奶牛中的 暴发 所证实的那样,有 71 例确诊的人类感染。虽然人与人之间的 传播 尚未有记录,但有限的人类现有免疫力以及人类感染进化障碍的不确定性使 H5N1 成为潜在的大流行威胁。将 H5N1 的检测能力扩大到目前有限的范围之外对于应对潜在的 暴发 至关重要。因此,我们开发并验证了一种双靶点逆转录 PCR 检测方法,用于对甲型流感阳性样本进行 H5 亚型分型。该检测针对甲型流感 (H5) 血凝素 (HA) 基因的两个区域。使用含有灭活牛 H5N1 病毒的人工样品,我们确定了单个样品(58 拷贝/mL;95% 置信区间 [CI]:50 至 66 拷贝/mL)和八个样品池(2,465 拷贝/mL;95% CI:1,969 至 3,756 拷贝/mL)的检测下限 95%。该测定显示出 100% 的阳性百分比一致性,在所有设计的 H5 阳性库(32/32 库;95% CI:89.1% 至 100.0%)中检测到 H5,其中设计的阳性样本范围为 500,000 至 3,906 拷贝/mL。我们观察到 H5 阴性、甲型流感阳性池中的阴性百分比一致性为 100%(32/32;95% CI:89.1% 至 100.0%)。尽管样本池降低了分析灵敏度,但在八个样本池中检测到了人类呼吸道感染中常见水平的 H5 病毒人为样本。这种方法有可能提高甲型流感 (H5) 的检测能力,同时保持足够的灵敏度,从而增强对潜在 暴发 的准备。重要性 禽流感 A(H5N1) 构成潜在的大流行威胁。H5N1 检测能力目前有限。我们验证了甲型流感 (H5) 亚型 RT-qPCR,用于测试合并样本以提高容量。该检测显示出足够的分析灵敏度,可根据先前的人类病例检测临床呼吸道感染。这种主动检测验证增强了 暴发 的准备能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于 禽流感 A(H5N1) 潜在的大流行威胁和当前检测能力的限制而增加其检测能力的需求。
设计与对象
该研究使用含有灭活牛 H5N1 病毒的人工样本进行,以验证对合并的甲型流感阳性样本的 H5 亚型的双靶点 RT-qPCR 检测。
核心方法
研究人员针对甲型流感 (H5) 的血凝素 (HA) 基因的两个区域开发了一种双靶点逆转录 PCR 检测方法,用于对合并样本进行 H5 亚型分析。他们使用人工样本来确定检测的检测限和一致率。
主要结果
该检测结果显示,H5 阳性和 H5 阴性池的阳性百分比和阴性百分比分别为 100%,单个样品的检测下限为 58 拷贝/mL,八个样品池的检测下限为 2,465 拷贝/mL,检测下限为 95%。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明该测定对检测混合样本中的 H5N1 保持了足够的灵敏度,这可以增强对潜在 暴发 的准备。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种方法可以提高 H5N1 的检测能力,同时保持足够的灵敏度,这对于早期检测和应对潜在的 暴发 至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于人为的样本,可能无法完全代表现实世界的情况,并且证据基于仅抽象的审查,导致对研究结果的可信度中等。

Validation of sample pooling for high-throughput RT-qPCR subtyping of avian influenza A(H5)

Authors: Grant P. Higerd-Rusli, Seth A. Hoffman, Abraar Karan, ChunHong Huang, Malaya K. Sahoo, Ingrid E. A. Morante, Matthew M. Hernandez, Benjamin A. Pinsky
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) is a global health threat due to its high historical virulence and increasing mammalian host range, as demonstrated by the 2024–2025 outbreak among U.S. dairy cattle, with 71 confirmed human infections. While human-to-human transmission has not yet been documented, limited pre-existing human immunity, and uncertainty about evolutionary barriers to human infection make H5N1 a potential pandemic threat. Expanding testing capacity for H5N1 beyond its currently limited scope would be critical for response to potential outbreaks. Thus, we developed and validated a dual-target reverse transcription PCR assay for H5 subtyping of pooled influenza A-positive samples. The assay targets two regions of the hemagglutinin (HA) gene of influenza A(H5). Using contrived samples containing inactivated bovine H5N1 virus, we determined the assay’s 95% lower limit of detection for individual samples (58 copies/mL; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 50 to 66 copies/mL) and eight-sample pools (2,465 copies/mL; 95% CI: 1,969 to 3,756 copies/mL). The assay demonstrated 100% positive percent agreement, detecting H5 in all contrived H5-positive pools (32/32 pools; 95% CI: 89.1% to 100.0%) where the contrived positive samples ranged from 500,000 to 3,906 copies/mL. We observed 100% negative percent agreement in H5-negative, influenza A-positive pools (32/32; 95% CI: 89.1% to 100.0%). Though sample pooling reduced analytical sensitivity, contrived samples with H5 virus present at levels typically found in human respiratory infections were detected in eight-sample pools. This approach has the potential to increase testing capacity for influenza A (H5) while maintaining adequate sensitivity, enhancing preparedness for potential outbreaks. IMPORTANCE Avian influenza A(H5N1) poses a potential pandemic threat. H5N1 testing capacity is currently limited. We validated an influenza A (H5) subtyping RT-qPCR for testing pooled samples to increase capacity. The assay displays adequate analytical sensitivity to detect clinical respiratory infections based on prior human cases. This proactive assay validation enhances outbreak preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for increased testing capacity for avian influenza A(H5N1) due to its potential pandemic threat and the current limitations in testing capabilities.
Design and population
The study was conducted using contrived samples containing inactivated bovine H5N1 virus to validate a dual-target RT-qPCR assay for H5 subtyping of pooled influenza A-positive samples.
Core methods
The researchers developed a dual-target reverse transcription PCR assay for H5 subtyping of pooled samples, targeting two regions of the hemagglutinin (HA) gene of influenza A(H5). They used contrived samples to determine the assay’s limit of detection and agreement rates.
Main results
The assay demonstrated 100% positive and negative percent agreement for H5-positive and H5-negative pools, respectively, with a 95% lower limit of detection for individual samples at 58 copies/mL and for eight-sample pools at 2,465 copies/mL.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating that the assay maintains adequate sensitivity for detecting H5N1 in pooled samples, which could enhance preparedness for potential outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this method can increase testing capacity for H5N1 while maintaining sufficient sensitivity, which is crucial for early detection and response to potential outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to contrived samples, which may not fully represent real-world conditions, and the evidence is based on an abstract-only review, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒在人呼吸道和牛乳腺上皮细胞中的细胞向性

Cellular tropism of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses in human respiratory and bovine mammary epithelial cells
作者： Nicole Brock, Hui Zeng, Claudia Pappas, Troy J. Kieran, Xiangjie Sun, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza, Terrence M. Tumpey, Taronna R. Maines, Jessica A. Belser
摘要中文翻译摘要 最近 HPAI A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒在美国奶牛中的引入和传播证明了它们适应新哺乳动物 宿主 的能力。尽管多种 A(H5N1) 基因型已引起人类感染,但在牛中仅检测到两种分支 2.3.4.4b 基因型(B3.13 和 D1.1),这表明分支或基因型特异性向性。了解这些病毒如何在人类呼吸道和牛乳腺上皮细胞中复制对于评估病毒 进化、哺乳动物适应和相关公共卫生风险非常重要。在这里,我们表征了代表 B3.13、D1.1 和 B3.2 基因型的 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 人类分离株、A(H5N1) clade 1 病毒和 A(H1N1)pdm09 毒株感染的人支气管上皮 (Calu-3) 细胞和牛乳腺上皮 (MAC-T) 细胞中的复制动力学和免疫基因表达。所有病毒都能在 Calu-3 细胞中高效复制,但与 37°C 相比,33°C 下的复制会延迟。进化枝 2.3.4.4b 病毒在 Calu-3 细胞中诱导 I 型干扰素的中等表达和促炎反应,但水平低于 A(H1N1)pdm09 和 A(H5N1) 进化枝 1 毒株。MAC-T 细胞中 clade 2.3.4.4b 基因型的复制和免疫激活存在显着差异。与其他 A(H5N1) 基因型或进化枝相比,B3.13 病毒获得了更高的滴度并引发了更强的先天 免疫应答 基因诱导。这些发现表明复制和 宿主 反应的基因型和细胞类型特异性差异,其中 B3.13 显示牛乳腺细胞的向性增强,这与可能提高人畜共患病风险的适应一致。我们的结果强调了 宿主 细胞类型和不同病毒分离株之间的比较研究的价值,为新兴流感病毒的风险评估提供信息。重要性甲型流感病毒通过多种因素跨越物种屏障,包括结合并感染受许可细胞的能力、高效复制以及调节 宿主 免疫应答 的能力。自 2024 年以来,A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒持续进化,感染了广泛的鸟类和哺乳动物物种,包括美国的牛,并引起零星人类感染。在这里,我们评估了一组从人类分离的 A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒,以评估它们在两种相关哺乳动物细胞类型(人支气管上皮细胞和牛乳腺上皮细胞)中的复制和 宿主 反应。虽然所有基因型都能在人支气管上皮细胞中有效复制,但只有 B3.13 病毒在牛乳腺上皮细胞中表现出强大的复制能力和广泛的 宿主 反应诱导。这些结果强调了 A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒之间 进化、组织向性和 宿主 适应性的变化,并强调需要继续 监测 和密切监测 B3.13 基因型病毒。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒在人类呼吸道和牛乳腺上皮细胞中的复制和 宿主 反应,因为它们传播到美国奶牛表明对新哺乳动物 宿主 的潜在适应。
设计与对象
该研究描述了感染代表 B3.13、D1.1 和 B3.2 基因型的 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 人类分离株、A(H5N1) clade 1 病毒和 A(H1N1)pdm09 毒株的人支气管上皮 (Calu-3) 细胞和牛乳腺上皮 (MAC-T) 细胞的复制动力学和免疫基因表达。
核心方法
研究人员通过用各种 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒感染 Calu-3 和 MAC-T 细胞并分析所得的病毒滴度和基因表达模式来评估复制和免疫激活。
主要结果
所有病毒都能在 Calu-3 细胞中高效复制,但与 37°C 相比,33°C 下的复制会延迟。进化枝 2.3.4.4b 病毒在 Calu-3 细胞中诱导 I 型干扰素的中等表达和促炎反应,但水平低于 A(H1N1)pdm09 和 A(H5N1) 进化枝 1 毒株。MAC-T 细胞中分支 2.3.4.4b 基因型的复制和免疫激活存在显着差异,与其他 A(H5N1) 基因型或分支相比,B3.13 病毒获得了更高的滴度并触发了更强的先天 免疫应答 基因诱导。
解释与创新
研究结果表明复制和 宿主 反应的基因型和细胞类型特异性差异,其中 B3.13 显示牛乳腺细胞的向性增强,这与可能提高人畜共患病风险的适应一致。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果强调了了解病毒 进化 和 宿主 适应的重要性,以告知新出现的流感病毒的风险评估,并强调需要继续研究 B3.13 基因型病毒的 监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外细胞培养模型,可能无法完全代表体内条件,并且由于缺乏临床或流行病学数据,证据不充分。

Cellular tropism of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses in human respiratory and bovine mammary epithelial cells

Authors: Nicole Brock, Hui Zeng, Claudia Pappas, Troy J. Kieran, Xiangjie Sun, Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza, Terrence M. Tumpey, Taronna R. Maines, Jessica A. Belser
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The recent introduction and spread of HPAI A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses to U.S. dairy cattle demonstrate their ability to adapt to new mammalian hosts. Although multiple A(H5N1) genotypes have caused human infections, only two clade 2.3.4.4b genotypes (B3.13 and D1.1) have been detected in cattle, suggesting clade- or genotype-specific tropism. Understanding how these viruses replicate in human airway and bovine mammary epithelial cells is important for assessing viral evolution, mammalian adaptation, and associated public health risks. Here, we characterized replication kinetics and immune gene expression in human bronchial epithelial (Calu-3) cells and bovine mammary epithelial (MAC-T) cells infected with A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b human isolates representing the B3.13, D1.1, and B3.2 genotypes, an A(H5N1) clade 1 virus, and an A(H1N1)pdm09 strain. All viruses replicated efficiently in Calu-3 cells, although replication was delayed at 33°C compared to 37°C. Clade 2.3.4.4b viruses induced moderate expression of type I interferon and proinflammatory response in Calu-3 cells, but at lower levels than the A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H5N1) clade 1 strains. Replication and immune activation varied markedly among clade 2.3.4.4b genotypes in MAC-T cells. B3.13 viruses achieved higher titers and triggered stronger induction of innate immune response genes than other A(H5N1) genotypes or clades. These findings indicate genotype- and cell type–specific differences in replication and host responses, with B3.13 showing enhanced tropism in bovine mammary cells, consistent with adaptation that may elevate zoonotic risk. Our results highlight the value of comparative studies across host cell types and diverse viral isolates to inform risk assessments for emerging influenza viruses. IMPORTANCE Influenza A viruses cross species barriers through a combination of factors, including the ability to bind to and infect permissive cells, replicate efficiently, and modulate host immune responses. Since 2024, A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses have continued to evolve, infecting a broad range of avian and mammalian species, including cattle in the United States, and causing sporadic human infections. Here, we evaluated a panel of A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses isolated from humans to assess their replication and host responses in two relevant mammalian cell types: human bronchial epithelial cells and bovine mammary gland epithelial cells. While all genotypes replicated efficiently in human bronchial epithelial cells, only B3.13 viruses showed strong replication and broad host response induction in bovine mammary epithelial cells. These results underscore variation in evolution, tissue tropism, and host adaptation among A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses and highlight the need for continued surveillance and close monitoring of B3.13 genotype viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the replication and host responses of A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses in human respiratory and bovine mammary epithelial cells, as their spread to U.S. dairy cattle indicates potential adaptation to new mammalian hosts.
Design and population
The study characterized replication kinetics and immune gene expression in human bronchial epithelial (Calu-3) cells and bovine mammary epithelial (MAC-T) cells infected with A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b human isolates representing the B3.13, D1.1, and B3.2 genotypes, an A(H5N1) clade 1 virus, and an A(H1N1)pdm09 strain.
Core methods
The researchers evaluated replication and immune activation by infecting Calu-3 and MAC-T cells with various A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b viruses and analyzing the resulting viral titers and gene expression patterns.
Main results
All viruses replicated efficiently in Calu-3 cells, although replication was delayed at 33°C compared to 37°C. Clade 2.3.4.4b viruses induced moderate expression of type I interferon and proinflammatory response in Calu-3 cells, but at lower levels than the A(H1N1)pdm09 and A(H5N1) clade 1 strains. Replication and immune activation varied markedly among clade 2.3.4.4b genotypes in MAC-T cells, with B3.13 viruses achieving higher titers and triggering stronger induction of innate immune response genes than other A(H5N1) genotypes or clades.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate genotype- and cell type–specific differences in replication and host responses, with B3.13 showing enhanced tropism in bovine mammary cells, consistent with adaptation that may elevate zoonotic risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the importance of understanding viral evolution and host adaptation to inform risk assessments for emerging influenza viruses and underscore the need for continued surveillance of B3.13 genotype viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro cell culture models, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions, and the evidence is moderate due to the absence of clinical or epidemiological data.

环境水平的异生素在体外以谱系和亚型依赖性方式调节流感病毒复制。

Xenobiotics at environmental levels modulate influenza virus replication in a lineage- and subtype-dependent manner in vitro.
作者： Younes Aftabi, B. Paige Lawrence, Sasan Fereidouni, Younes Aftabı
摘要中文翻译环境相关浓度的芳烃受体 (AhR) 活性污染物对低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 复制的影响仍未得到很好的解决,特别是在现实的共同暴露场景下。在这里,我们量化了单一和混合 AhR 激动剂如何在现场相关浓度下调节禽类成纤维细胞中多种 LPAIV 亚型的复制。将鸡 (DF-1) 和鸭 (CCL-141) 成纤维细胞暴露于 9-氯菲 (0.1-50 nM)、PCB126 (0.01-10 nM) 和 TCDD (0.001-1 nM),有或没有内源性 AhR 配体 6-甲酰基吲哚并[3,2-b]咔唑 (FICZ),随后感染四种 LPAIV亚型(H16N3、H9N2、H7N3、H5N3)。通过 qPCR (ΔCt) 定量病毒复制。阶乘模型强调 宿主 谱系 × 病毒亚型相互作用以及共享浓度窗口内的混合物与单一比较。宿主 谱系 × 病毒亚型相互作用解释了最大的方差份额。单剂效应高度依赖于环境;仅在感染 H7N3 的 DF-1 细胞中观察到 PCB126 的统计支持的 抗病毒药物 活性 (q = 0.006)。相比之下,三种化合物污染物混合物显着增强了感染 H7N3 的 CCL-141 细胞的复制 (q = 3.8 × 10^-4),而 FICZ 共同暴露时未观察到这种支持趋势。在测试的环境合理浓度窗口内,浓度匹配的最高单剂比较经常产生低于评估比较中最强匹配单剂响应的混合物响应。在测试的浓度窗口内,反应被分级并且缺乏明确的阈值。总体而言,AhR 活性异生素可以以 宿主 和亚型特异性方式调节 LPAIV 复制,这凸显了对混合物感知、环境依赖的生态感染风险评估的需要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
环境相关浓度的芳烃受体 (AhR) 活性污染物对低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 复制的影响仍未得到很好的解决,特别是在现实的共同暴露场景下。
设计与对象
在这里,我们量化了单一和混合 AhR 激动剂如何在现场相关浓度下调节禽类成纤维细胞中多种 LPAIV 亚型的复制。
核心方法
将鸡 (DF-1) 和鸭 (CCL-141) 成纤维细胞暴露于 9-氯菲 (0.1-50 nM)、PCB126 (0.01-10 nM) 和 TCDD (0.001-1 nM),有或没有内源性 AhR 配体 6-甲酰基吲哚并[3,2-b]咔唑 (FICZ),随后感染四种 LPAIV亚型(H16N3、H9N2、H7N3、H5N3)。
主要结果
通过 qPCR (ΔCt) 定量病毒复制。
解释与创新
阶乘模型强调 宿主 谱系 × 病毒亚型相互作用以及共享浓度窗口内的混合物与单一比较。
科研与公卫意义
总体而言,AhR 活性异生素可以以 宿主 和亚型特异性方式调节 LPAIV 复制,这凸显了对混合物感知、环境依赖的生态感染风险评估的需要。
局限与证据强度
单剂效应高度依赖于环境;仅在感染 H7N3 的 DF-1 细胞中观察到 PCB126 的统计支持的 抗病毒药物 活性 (q = 0.006)。

Xenobiotics at environmental levels modulate influenza virus replication in a lineage- and subtype-dependent manner in vitro.

Authors: Younes Aftabi, B. Paige Lawrence, Sasan Fereidouni, Younes Aftabı
Original abstract (English)
The influence of environmentally relevant concentrations of aryl hydrocarbon receptor (AhR)-active pollutants on low-pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) replication remains poorly resolved, particularly under realistic co-exposure scenarios. Here, we quantified how single and mixed AhR agonists modulate replication of multiple LPAIV subtypes in avian fibroblasts at field-relevant concentrations. Chicken (DF-1) and duck (CCL-141) fibroblasts were exposed to 9-chlorophenanthrene (0.1-50 nM), PCB126 (0.01-10 nM), and TCDD (0.001-1 nM), with or without the endogenous AhR ligand 6-formylindolo[3,2-b]carbazole (FICZ), and subsequently infected with four LPAIV subtypes (H16N3, H9N2, H7N3, H5N3). Viral replication was quantified by qPCR (ΔCt). Factorial models emphasized host lineage × virus subtype interactions and mixture-versus-single comparisons across a shared concentration window. Host lineage × virus subtype interactions explained the largest share of variance. Single-agent effects were highly context-dependent; statistically supported antiviral activity was observed only for PCB126 in DF-1 cells infected with H7N3 (q = 0.006). In contrast, a three-compound pollutant mixture significantly enhanced replication in CCL-141 cells infected with H7N3 (q = 3.8 × 10^-4), whereas this supported trend was not observed with FICZ co-exposure. Within the tested environmentally plausible concentration window, concentration-matched Highest Single Agent comparisons more often yielded mixture responses below the strongest matched single-agent response among the evaluated comparisons. Within the tested concentration window, responses were graded and lacked clear thresholds. Overall, AhR-active xenobiotics can tune LPAIV replication in a host- and subtype-specific manner, highlighting the need for mixture-aware, context-dependent ecological infection-risk assessment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The influence of environmentally relevant concentrations of aryl hydrocarbon receptor (AhR)-active pollutants on low-pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) replication remains poorly resolved, particularly under realistic co-exposure scenarios.
Design and population

Main results
Viral replication was quantified by qPCR (ΔCt).
Interpretation and novelty
Factorial models emphasized host lineage × virus subtype interactions and mixture-versus-single comparisons across a shared concentration window.
Scientific and public-health significance

爱尔兰 禽流感 迁移暴露风险的应用和评估(2013-2025)

Application and evaluation of a migratory exposure risk for avian influenza in Ireland (2013–2025)
作者： Joyce van Leeuwen, Michel Counotte, Bart H.P. van den Borne, Andrew W. Byrne
摘要中文翻译禽流感 (AI) 是一种感染 野生鸟类 和家禽的人畜共患病毒。AI可以通过野生鸟类迁徙传播,尤其是通过水禽。在爱尔兰岛上,大多数家禽中的 暴发 都与 野生鸟类 接触有关。因此,通过 野生鸟类 预测人工智能的暴露情况有助于指导措施和疾病预防。我们基于 野生鸟类 短距离移动和季节性迁徙,回顾性地应用随机模拟模型来估计 2013 年至 2025 年爱尔兰因迁徙介导的人工智能暴露风险。鉴于已知的鸟类迁徙路径,迁徙路线包括欧洲大陆和冰岛作为人工智能可能的来源种群和进入路线。该模型估计了年度迁移介导的暴露风险,并确定了 2020 年、2021 年的峰值,以及 2025 年秋季出现的一个特别高的峰值。每月迁移介导的暴露风险与观察到的 暴发 之间存在时间相关性。然而,与观察到的 暴发 数量相比,该模型预测的迁移介导的暴露风险导致建模“入侵”的数量始终较高,这表明需要将迁移介导的暴露风险模型与岛内流行病模型联系起来。优选地,包括随后感染和检测的概率。这项研究强调了应用本地定制风险模型的好处,但也强调了未来工作需要“实时”整理和分析数据以改进此类工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 (AI) 是一种感染 野生鸟类 和家禽的人畜共患病毒。AI可以通过野生鸟类迁徙传播,尤其是通过水禽。
设计与对象
在爱尔兰岛上,大多数家禽中的 暴发 都与 野生鸟类 接触有关。该模型估计了年度移民介导的暴露风险,并确定了 2020 年、2021 年的峰值,以及 2025 年秋季出现的一个特别高的峰值。
核心方法
我们基于 野生鸟类 短距离移动和季节性迁徙,回顾性地应用随机模拟模型来估计 2013 年至 2025 年爱尔兰因迁徙介导的人工智能暴露风险。鉴于已知的鸟类迁徙路径,迁徙路线包括欧洲大陆和冰岛作为人工智能可能的来源种群和进入路线。
主要结果
每月迁移介导的暴露风险与观察到的 暴发 之间存在时间相关性。
解释与创新
相对于观察到的 暴发 数量,该模型预测的迁移介导的暴露风险导致建模的“入侵”数量始终较高,这表明需要将迁移介导的暴露风险模型与岛内流行病模型联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究强调了应用本地定制风险模型的好处,但也强调了未来工作需要“实时”整理和分析数据以改进此类工具。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Application and evaluation of a migratory exposure risk for avian influenza in Ireland (2013–2025)

Authors: Joyce van Leeuwen, Michel Counotte, Bart H.P. van den Borne, Andrew W. Byrne
Original abstract (English)
Avian Influenza (AI) is a zoonotic virus infecting wild birds and poultry. AI can be spread via wild bird migration, especially by waterfowl. On the island of Ireland, most outbreaks in poultry are linked to wild bird contact. Therefore, predicting the exposure to AI through wild birds could help with directing measures and disease preparedness. We retrospectively applied a stochastic simulation model to estimate the migration-mediated exposure risk of AI in Ireland from 2013 to 2025, based on wild bird short distance movements and seasonal migration. Migration routes include the continent of Europe and Iceland as possible source populations and entry routes of AI, given known bird migration pathways. The model estimated the yearly migration-mediated exposure risk and identified peaks in 2020, 2021, and a particular high emerging peak in autumn of 2025. There was a temporal correlation between migration-mediated exposure risk per month with observed outbreaks. However, the predicted migration-mediated exposure risk for the model resulted in consistently higher number of modelled “incursions” relative to the number of outbreaks observed – indicating a need for migration-mediated exposure risk models to be linked to a within-island epidemic model. Preferably one that includes the probability of subsequent infections and detection. This study highlights a benefit for the application of locally tailored risk models, but also the need for future work to collate and analyse data in “real-time” to improve such tools.

相关灭活 疫苗 针对 高致病性禽流感 和鸡新城疫的开发、免疫原性和保护功效

Development, Immunogenicity and Protective Efficacy of an Associated Inactivated Vaccine Against Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza and Newcastle Disease in Chickens
作者： Yeldos Myrzakhmetov, Nurika Assanzhanova, Sholpan Ryskeldinova, Aigerim Mailybayeva, Aina Muratovna Sagimbayeva, Yerken Kozhamkulov, Y. S. Yamanova, Rassul Sidikhov, Nurlan S. Kozhabergenov, Bekbolat Usserbayev
摘要中文翻译背景:高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N8(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)和强毒新城疫病毒 (NDV) 基因型 VII 的同时传播给家禽业造成累积风险,导致单价 疫苗接种 不足。本研究旨在开发和评估基于当地流行毒株的针对 AIV H5N8 和 NDV 基因型 VII 的组合灭活 疫苗。方法:使用 MontanideTM ISA 78 VG 将 疫苗 配制为油包水乳液 (30:70)。评估包括理化特性、鸡的安全性评估、通过血凝抑制(HI)测试的免疫原性评估以及强毒分离株攻击后的保护功效。结果:该制剂稳定性高,安全性高,无不良反应。免疫后 ​​28 天,疫苗 诱导了强烈、平衡的体液反应,针对 NDV 的抗体滴度达到 9.10 ± 0.23 log2,针对 AIV 的抗体滴度达到 9.00 ± 0.26 log2,表明没有抗原干扰。攻击后,接种组表现出 100% 的临床保护和存活率,而对照组的 死亡率 为 100%。此外,疫苗接种 显着减少了病毒脱落,限制了水平传播。结论:所开发的组合灭活疫苗具有较高的安全性、稳定性和保护功效。它是流行地区全面特异性预防的有前途的候选药物,可有效限制水平病原体 传播 并减轻家禽生产的经济损失。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N8(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)和强毒新城疫病毒 (NDV) 基因型 VII 的同时传播给家禽业造成累积风险,导致单价 疫苗接种 不足。

核心方法
使用 MontanideTM ISA 78 VG 将 疫苗 配制为油包水乳液 (30:70)。

解释与创新
所开发的组合灭活疫苗具有较高的安全性、稳定性和保护功效。

它是流行地区全面特异性预防的有前途的候选药物,可有效限制水平病原体 传播 并减轻家禽生产的经济损失。
局限与证据强度

Development, Immunogenicity and Protective Efficacy of an Associated Inactivated Vaccine Against Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza and Newcastle Disease in Chickens

Authors: Yeldos Myrzakhmetov, Nurika Assanzhanova, Sholpan Ryskeldinova, Aigerim Mailybayeva, Aina Muratovna Sagimbayeva, Yerken Kozhamkulov, Y. S. Yamanova, Rassul Sidikhov, Nurlan S. Kozhabergenov, Bekbolat Usserbayev
Original abstract (English)
Background: The simultaneous circulation of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N8 (clade 2.3.4.4b) and virulent Newcastle disease virus (NDV) genotype VII creates a cumulative risk for the poultry industry, rendering monovalent vaccination insufficient. This study aimed to develop and evaluate a combined inactivated vaccine against AIV H5N8 and NDV genotype VII based on local endemic strains. Methods: The vaccine was formulated as a water-in-oil emulsion (30:70) using MontanideTM ISA 78 VG. Evaluation included physicochemical characterization, safety assessment in chickens, immunogenicity evaluation via the hemagglutination inhibition (HI) test, and protective efficacy following challenge with virulent isolates. Results: The formulation demonstrated high stability and complete safety without adverse reactions. By 28 days post-immunization, the vaccine induced a robust, balanced humoral response with antibody titers reaching 9.10 ± 0.23 log2 against NDV and 9.00 ± 0.26 log2 against AIV, indicating no antigenic interference. Upon challenge, the vaccinated group exhibited 100% clinical protection and survival, compared to 100% mortality in controls. Furthermore, vaccination significantly reduced viral shedding, limiting horizontal spread. Conclusions: The developed combined inactivated vaccine demonstrates high safety, stability, and protective efficacy. It represents a promising candidate for comprehensive specific prophylaxis in endemic regions, effectively limiting horizontal pathogen transmission and mitigating economic losses in poultry production.
View research seven-element analysis

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

冲突阻碍了对动物运动的研究。

Conflicts hinder research into animal movements.
作者： Víctor Martín-Vélez, Joan Navarro, Isabel Afán, Tomas Montalvo, Andy J Green
摘要中文翻译卫星追踪彻底改变了我们对动物迁徙的理解,但其可靠性越来越依赖于野生动物经常出没地区的地缘政治稳定性。在这里,我们表明,在东欧持续冲突期间,军事对全球导航卫星系统(GNSS)的干扰严重影响了基于全球定位系统(GPS)的黑头鸥(Chroicocephalus rodibundus)跟踪数据的准确性。2024-2025 年,GPS 轨迹显示出不稳定、低质量且在地理上不可信的位置,与已知的电子战区域重合。这些不准确之处阻碍了定位繁殖群、确定关键中途停留栖息地以及评估候鸟带来的疾病 传播 风险的工作,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。我们的研究结果说明了现代冲突如何将其影响扩展到生态研究基础设施,呼吁采取系统的纠正方法和国际协调,以在地缘政治不稳定的世界中保障运动生态学研究和同一个健康模型的稳健性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
卫星追踪彻底改变了我们对动物迁徙的理解,但其可靠性越来越依赖于野生动物经常出没地区的地缘政治稳定性。
设计与对象
在这里,我们表明,在东欧持续冲突期间,军事对全球导航卫星系统(GNSS)的干扰严重影响了基于全球定位系统(GPS)的黑头鸥(Chroicocephalus rodibundus)跟踪数据的准确性。
核心方法
2024-2025 年,GPS 轨迹显示出不稳定、低质量且在地理上不可信的位置,与已知的电子战区域重合。
主要结果
这些不准确之处阻碍了定位繁殖群、确定关键中途停留栖息地以及评估候鸟带来的疾病 传播 风险的工作,特别是 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果说明了现代冲突如何将其影响扩展到生态研究基础设施,呼吁采取系统的纠正方法和国际协调,以在地缘政治不稳定的世界中保障运动生态学研究和同一个健康模型的稳健性。
科研与公卫意义
受损的跟踪数据影响了关键的生态和公共卫生研究,包括对候鸟疾病 传播 风险的评估,特别是 HPAI H5N1 等人畜共患病的风险。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于特定冲突地区的 GPS 轨迹分析,限制了对其他地区或时间段的普遍适用性;然而,对干扰的直接观察为报告的影响提供了有力的证据。

Conflicts hinder research into animal movements.

Authors: Víctor Martín-Vélez, Joan Navarro, Isabel Afán, Tomas Montalvo, Andy J Green
Original abstract (English)
Satellite tracking has revolutionized our understanding of animal migration, yet its reliability increasingly depends on the geopolitical stability of the regions frequented by wildlife. Here, we show that military-induced interference with global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) during ongoing conflicts in Eastern Europe has severely compromised the accuracy of global positioning systems (GPS)-based tracking data for black-headed gulls (Chroicocephalus ridibundus). In 2024-2025, GPS trajectories revealed erratic, low-quality, and geographically implausible positions coinciding with known zones of electronic warfare. These inaccuracies hinder efforts to locate breeding colonies, identify key stopover habitats, and assess disease transmission risks posed by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1. Our findings illustrate how modern conflicts now extend their impact into ecological research infrastructures, calling for systematic correction methods and international coordination to safeguard the robustness of movement ecology studies and One Health models in a geopolitically unstable world.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Satellite tracking has revolutionized our understanding of animal migration, yet its reliability increasingly depends on the geopolitical stability of the regions frequented by wildlife.
Design and population
Here, we show that military-induced interference with global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) during ongoing conflicts in Eastern Europe has severely compromised the accuracy of global positioning systems (GPS)-based tracking data for black-headed gulls (Chroicocephalus ridibundus).
Core methods
In 2024-2025, GPS trajectories revealed erratic, low-quality, and geographically implausible positions coinciding with known zones of electronic warfare.
Main results
These inaccuracies hinder efforts to locate breeding colonies, identify key stopover habitats, and assess disease transmission risks posed by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings illustrate how modern conflicts now extend their impact into ecological research infrastructures, calling for systematic correction methods and international coordination to safeguard the robustness of movement ecology studies and One Health models in a geopolitically unstable world.
Scientific and public-health significance
The compromised tracking data affects critical ecological and public health research, including the assessment of disease transmission risks by migratory birds, particularly for zoonoses like HPAI H5N1.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on GPS trajectory analysis during specific conflict zones, limiting generalizability to other regions or time periods; however, the direct observation of interference provides strong evidence for the reported impact.

中国三省家禽相关职业群体对禽流感的知识、态度和实践

Knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding avian influenza among poultry-related occupational groups in three provinces of China
作者： Huimin Li, Sai Hou, Lijun Zhou, Weitao He, Wei Gao, Wenqing Bai, Ruiqi Ren, Dan Li, Chao Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 近年来,禽流感 暴发 的全球风险显着增加,人类感染病例频发,对家禽业和公众健康构成重大威胁。由于与家禽长期密切接触,家禽业的职业群体面临特别高的感染风险。本研究旨在明确不同职业类别对禽流感知识、态度和实践(KAP)的差异,查明防控薄弱环节,为有针对性的干预策略提供科学依据。我们于2025年5月至2025年7月对中国安徽、四川、广西三省从事家禽相关职业的1,362名从事家禽相关职业的个体进行了横断面调查。通过电子问卷收集了禽流感相关知识、态度和实践(KAP)数据。使用 SPSS 27.0 进行统计分析,采用描述性统计、组间比较的卡方检验以及多元逻辑回归来确定与 KAP 水平相关的因素。共回收有效问卷1194份。调查对象KAP总体合格率为48.16%,其中知识知晓率最低(33.84%),积极态度率为83.42%,积极实践率为41.46%。多变量分析确定了影响知识、态度和实践的不同因素。在知识方面,商业农场工人(aOR = 1.87,95%CI:1.24–2.81)和散养农民(aOR = 2.40,95%CI:1.37–4.21)的知识水平高于活禽市场工人,城市居民(aOR = 1.49,95%CI:1.06–2.09)、受教育程度较高、无慢性病(aOR = 1.94,95%CI:1.30–2.91)也预测了更好的知识。对于态度,商业农场工人(aOR=3.02,95%CI:1.67-5.46)和城市居民(aOR=2.84,95%CI:1.79-4.51)更为积极。就实践而言,广西工人的表现优于安徽工人(aOR = 1.72,95%CI:1.06–2.79);商业农场工人(aOR = 7.01,95%CI:4.55–10.80)和屠宰场工人(aOR = 6.33,95%CI:3.63–11.05)表现优于活禽市场工人,而散养农民的表现较差(aOR = 0.40,95%CI:0.22–0.74);女性表现出比男性更好的实践(aOR = 1.33,95% CI:1.01–1.77)。此外,移动互联网(59.55%)和电视/广播(50.42%)是所有职业群体的主要信息来源。研究结果揭示了一种肤浅的知识和积极态度的模式,这些模式并不能转化为有效的保护措施。活禽市场工作人员、散养农民、受教育程度较低的个人以及居住在农村地区的人对 禽流感 的了解较少,而男性对保护措施的采用率较低。这些人群应优先作为干预的重点目标。建议通过新媒体平台开展有针对性的健康传播,提高干预措施的覆盖面和有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了 禽流感 禽流感 不断上升的全球风险及其对家禽和公共卫生的威胁,并指出人类感染频繁。报告强调,家禽业的职业群体由于长期与家禽密切接触而具有特别高的感染风险。
设计与对象
2025 年 5 月至 7 月,对中国安徽、四川和广西三省从事家禽相关职业的 1,362 名个人进行了横断面调查。通过电子问卷收集数据,以评估有关 禽流感 的知识、态度和实践 (KAP)。
核心方法
使用 SPSS 27.0 进行统计分析,包括描述性统计、组间比较的卡方检验以及多元逻辑回归以确定与 KAP 水平相关的因素。有效问卷总数为1,194份。
主要结果
总体KAP合格率为48.16%,其中知识认知率为33.84%,积极态度率为83.42%,积极实践率为41.46%。商业农场工人和后院农民的知识水平高于活禽市场工人,而商业农场工人和屠宰场工人则表现出更好的实践。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了一种肤浅的知识和积极态度的模式,这些模式并不能转化为有效的保护措施。该研究确定了影响 KAP 水平的不同职业和人口因素,强调有针对性的干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果突出了干预的重点人群,包括活禽市场工人、散养农民、教育程度较低的个人、农村居民和男性,他们的知识和实践较差。建议通过新媒体平台进行有针对性的健康传播,以提高干预效果。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法进行因果推断,并且自我报告的 KAP 数据中存在潜在的反应偏差。由于这些方法学限制和对单一调查周期的依赖,证据强度中等。

Knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding avian influenza among poultry-related occupational groups in three provinces of China

Authors: Huimin Li, Sai Hou, Lijun Zhou, Weitao He, Wei Gao, Wenqing Bai, Ruiqi Ren, Dan Li, Chao Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The global risk of avian influenza outbreaks has increased significantly in recent years, posing substantial threats to both the poultry industry and public health due to frequent human infection cases. Occupational groups within the poultry industry face particularly high infection risks because of prolonged close contact with poultry. This study aimed to identify differences in knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) regarding avian influenza among various occupational categories, pinpoint weaknesses in prevention and control, and provide scientific evidence to support targeted intervention strategies. We conducted a cross-sectional survey using stratified random sampling among 1,362 individuals engaged in poultry-related occupations in Anhui, Sichuan, and Guangxi provinces, China, from May to July 2025. Data on avian influenza-related knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) were collected through electronic questionnaires. Statistical analyses were performed using SPSS 27.0, employing descriptive statistics, chi-square tests for group comparisons, and multivariate logistic regression to identify factors associated with KAP levels. A total of 1,194 valid questionnaires were collected. The overall KAP qualification rate among survey participants was 48.16%, with knowledge awareness being the lowest (33.84%), the positive attitude rate was 83.42%, and the positive practice rate was 41.46%. Multivariate analysis identified distinct factors influencing knowledge, attitude, and practice. Regarding knowledge, commercial farm workers (aOR = 1.87,95%CI:1.24–2.81) and backyard farmers(aOR = 2.40,95%CI:1.37–4.21) had higher levels than live poultry market workers, with urban residents(aOR = 1.49,95%CI:1.06–2.09), higher education, and absence of chronic disease (aOR = 1.94,95%CI:1.30–2.91) also predicting better knowledge. For attitude, commercial farm workers (aOR = 3.02,95%CI:1.67–5.46) and urban residents (aOR = 2.84,95%CI:1.79–4.51) were more positive. For practice, workers in Guangxi outperformed those in Anhui (aOR = 1.72,95%CI:1.06–2.79); commercial farm workers (aOR = 7.01,95%CI:4.55–10.80) and slaughterhouse workers (aOR = 6.33,95%CI:3.63–11.05) outperformed live poultry market workers, while backyard farmers showed poorer practices (aOR = 0.40,95%CI:0.22–0.74); females exhibited better practices than males (aOR = 1.33,95% CI:1.01–1.77). Furthermore, mobile internet (59.55%) and television/radio (50.42%) were the primary sources of information across all occupational groups. The findings reveal a pattern of superficial knowledge, and positive attitudes that do not translate into effective protective practices. Live poultry market workers, backyard farmers, individuals with lower education levels, and those residing in rural areas demonstrated poorer knowledge of avian influenza, while males exhibited lower adoption rates of protective practices. These populations should be prioritized as key targets for intervention. It is recommended to implement targeted health communication through new media platforms to enhance the coverage and effectiveness of intervention measures.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the rising global risk of avian influenza outbreaks and their threats to poultry and public health, noting frequent human infections. It highlights occupational groups in the poultry industry as having particularly high infection risks due to prolonged close contact with poultry.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted among 1,362 individuals engaged in poultry-related occupations in Anhui, Sichuan, and Guangxi provinces, China, from May to July 2025. Data were collected via electronic questionnaires to assess knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) regarding avian influenza.
Core methods
Statistical analyses were performed using SPSS 27.0, including descriptive statistics, chi-square tests for group comparisons, and multivariate logistic regression to identify factors associated with KAP levels. Valid questionnaires totaled 1,194.
Main results
The overall KAP qualification rate was 48.16%, with knowledge awareness at 33.84%, positive attitude rate at 83.42%, and positive practice rate at 41.46%. Commercial farm workers and backyard farmers had higher knowledge levels than live poultry market workers, while commercial farm workers and slaughterhouse workers showed better practices.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a pattern of superficial knowledge and positive attitudes that do not translate into effective protective practices. The study identifies distinct occupational and demographic factors influencing KAP levels, emphasizing the need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight key populations for intervention, including live poultry market workers, backyard farmers, individuals with lower education, rural residents, and males, who demonstrated poorer knowledge and practices. Targeted health communication via new media platforms is recommended to enhance intervention effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which precludes causal inference, and potential response bias in self-reported KAP data. The evidence strength is moderate due to these methodological constraints and the reliance on a single survey period.

酸、pH 值和孵育时间对 禽流感 病毒在原奶中持久性的影响

The impact of acids, pH, and incubation time on avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk
作者： Harrell TL, Shwani A, Suarez DL
摘要中文翻译2024 年 3 月,高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 进化枝 2.3.4.4。b 在奶牛中检测到 H5N1。自检测以来,该病毒已在 17 个州传播,感染了 1000 多个奶牛群,引起了乳制品行业对其对奶牛的影响、与牛奶质量和生产相关的风险以及最终对人类的风险的担忧。研究表明,巴氏灭菌足以灭活牛奶中存在的 HPAIV,使其可供人类安全食用。然而,经常食用并用于制作奶酪、酸奶和开菲尔的未经巴氏消毒的生奶是一个公共健康问题。我们用乙酸、丙酸、乳酸或柠檬酸酸化原料奶,这一过程类似于生产发酵乳产品的初始阶段,将 pH 值调至 5;或用乙酸将其酸化至 pH 6、5 或 4。随后将每个样品掺入低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV),并在室温下孵育长达 24 小时,然后接种到 10 日龄的无特定病原体的胚胎鸡蛋中。每天评估胚胎的活力,并使用尿囊液的血凝测定来确认存活的 LPAIV 的存在。活病毒的减少与时间和 pH 值显着相关,但与测试的特定酸类型无关。pH 4 和 5 的酸 治疗 随着时间的推移逐渐降低活 LPAIV 水平,在 24 小时观察到最明显的失活。pH 值为 6 的样品病毒活力几乎没有降低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 在 17 个州的奶牛中传播,引发了人们对未经高温消毒的原料奶的牛奶质量、生产和人类健康风险的担忧,因此需要进行这项研究。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用各种酸将生奶酸化至不同的 pH 值,添加低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV),并在室温下孵育长达 24 小时,然后接种到 10 日龄的无特定病原体的胚胎鸡蛋中。
核心方法
将样品用乙酸、丙酸、乳酸或柠檬酸酸化至 pH 5,或用乙酸酸化至 pH 6、5 或 4,加入 LPAIV,孵育,然后接种到含胚鸡蛋中,每天通过血凝测定评估活力。
主要结果
活病毒的减少与时间和 pH 值显着相关,但与测试的酸的具体类型无关;pH 6 的样品显示病毒活力几乎没有降低,而 pH 4 和 5 的酸 治疗 随着时间的推移逐渐降低活的 LPAIV 水平。
解释与创新
作者解释说,酸 治疗,特别是在较低 pH 水平下,可以有效灭活原料奶中的 LPAIV,为降低与未经巴氏消毒的乳制品相关的公共卫生风险提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为减少原奶中 禽流感 病毒持久性的潜在干预措施提供了信息,从而减轻发酵食品中使用的未经高温消毒的乳制品对人类健康的风险。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究是在受控实验室环境中使用胚胎鸡蛋进行的,这可能无法完全复制乳制品加工或人类消费的现实条件。

The impact of acids, pH, and incubation time on avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk

Authors: Harrell TL, Shwani A, Suarez DL
Original abstract (English)
In March 2024, highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4. b H5N1 was detected in dairy cattle. Since detection, the virus has spread across 17 states, infecting more than 1000 dairy herds, causing concern for the dairy industry regarding the effects on dairy cattle, the risk associated with milk quality and production, and ultimately its risk to humans. It has been shown that pasteurization is sufficient to inactivate HPAIV, if present in milk, making it safe for human consumption. However, unpasteurized raw milk that is routinely consumed and used to make cheese, yogurt, and kefir is a public health concern. We acidified raw milk, a process that is analogous to the preliminary phase of generating fermented milk products, with acetic, propionic, lactic, or citric acid to pH 5; or acidified it with acetic acid to pH 6, 5, or 4. Each sample was subsequently spiked with low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) and incubated at room temperature for up to 24 h before being inoculated into 10-day old specific-pathogen-free embryonating chicken eggs. Embryos were assessed daily for viability and hemagglutination assays on the allantoic fluid was used to confirm the presence of viable LPAIV. The reduction of viable virus was significantly correlated with both time and pH but not specific type of acid tested. An acid treatment at pH 4 and 5 progressively reduced viable LPAIV levels over time, with the most pronounced inactivation observed at 24 h. Samples at pH 6 had little reduction in virus viability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed due to the spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 in dairy cattle across 17 states, raising concerns about milk quality, production, and human health risks from unpasteurized raw milk.
Design and population
The study involved acidifying raw milk with various acids to different pH levels, spiking it with low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV), and incubating it at room temperature for up to 24 hours before inoculating into 10-day-old specific-pathogen-free embryonating chicken eggs.
Core methods
Samples were acidified with acetic, propionic, lactic, or citric acid to pH 5 or with acetic acid to pH 6, 5, or 4, spiked with LPAIV, incubated, and then inoculated into embryonating chicken eggs, with viability assessed daily via hemagglutination assays.
Main results
The reduction of viable virus was significantly correlated with both time and pH but not the specific type of acid tested; samples at pH 6 showed little reduction in virus viability, while acid treatment at pH 4 and 5 progressively reduced viable LPAIV levels over time.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that acid treatment, particularly at lower pH levels, can effectively inactivate LPAIV in raw milk, providing a novel approach to reduce public health risks associated with unpasteurized dairy products.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform potential interventions for reducing avian influenza virus persistence in raw milk, thereby mitigating risks to human health from unpasteurized dairy products used in fermented foods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study was conducted in a controlled laboratory setting using embryonating chicken eggs, which may not fully replicate real-world conditions in dairy processing or human consumption.

Journal: Research in Veterinary Science and Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-05  |  DOI: 10.25259/rvsm_3_2026  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 6, 2
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

尼日利亚部分活禽市场家禽处理人员对 高致病性禽流感 的风险因素、认知和知识

Risk Factors, Perception, and Knowledge of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza among Poultry Handlers in Selected Live Bird Markets in Nigeria
作者： Kayode Abraham Olawuyi, Olukayode Olugbenga Orole, Uwem Okon Edet, Foluke Grace Olawuyi, Nicodemus Mkpuma, Chukwudi Ofodile, Clement Meseko
摘要中文翻译目标:活禽市场 (LBM) 被认为是家禽传染性病原体 传播 的高风险区域,因为大量未经检查、疾病状况未知的混合家禽品种聚集在一起。目的是确定尼日利亚北部部分地区与 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) LBM 工人相关的知识、风险认知和生物安全实践方面的差距。材料和方法:使用结构化访谈问卷对 226 名 LBM 员工进行了一项横断面研究。使用描述性统计和二元逻辑回归分析有关社会人口统计、家禽处理实践、生物安全措施、知识和风险认知的数据。结果:所有受访者均为男性(平均年龄 40 ± 12.3 岁)。LBM 员工对 HPAI 的认识较高(>95%);然而,从事多种鸟类交易的供应商对其人畜共患潜力的了解很少(90%)。百分之四十五 (45%) 的供应商认为将病禽引入市场风险较低,而 42% 的供应商认为对于流感病毒 传播 风险较高。超过 70% 的摊贩定期清洁摊位和笼子,作为一项重要的生物安全措施。将病禽与健康禽类分开的供应商在市场上感染流感病毒 传播 的可能性较小(比值比 [OR]:0.214;95% 置信区间 [CI]:0.073–0.630)。使用个人防护设备和对所用车辆进行消毒与 LBM 内流感病毒 传播 风险降低相关(OR:0.534;95% 置信区间 [CI]:0.298–0.953;OR:0.584;95% CL:0.450–0.757)。结论:尽管人们的认识很高,但在人畜共患知识、生物安全失误和低风险认知方面仍存在严重差距。迫切需要有针对性的风险沟通和增强的生物安全协议来减轻这些 LBM 中的 HPAI 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过检查家禽处理者的知识、认知和生物安全实践来解决活禽市场中 禽流感 传播 的风险。由于大量未经检查、疾病状况不明的混合家禽品种聚集在一起,活禽市场被认为是家禽传染性病原体 传播 的高风险区域。
设计与对象
使用结构化访谈问卷对尼日利亚北部部分地区的 226 名 LBM 工人进行了一项横断面研究。所有受访者均为男性(平均年龄 40 ± 12.3 岁)。
核心方法
使用描述性统计和二元逻辑回归分析有关社会人口统计、家禽处理实践、生物安全措施、知识和风险认知的数据。
主要结果
LBM 员工对 HPAI 的认识较高(>95%);然而,人们对其人畜共患潜力的了解还很少。百分之四十五 (45%) 的供应商认为将病禽引入市场风险较低,而 42% 的供应商认为对于流感病毒 传播 风险较高。将病禽与健康禽类分开的供应商在市场上感染流感病毒 传播 的可能性较小(比值比 [OR]:0.214;95% 置信区间 [CI]:0.073–0.630)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管人们的认识很高,但人畜共患知识、生物安全失误和低风险认知方面仍存在严重差距。这项研究强调需要有针对性的风险沟通和增强的生物安全协议,以减轻这些 LBM 中的 HPAI 传播。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为有针对性的风险沟通策略和增强生物安全方案的制定提供信息,以降低活禽市场中 HPAI 传播 的风险,这对尼日利亚的公共卫生至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法确定因果关系。证据是温和的,因为它基于自我报告的数据和描述性统计。

Risk Factors, Perception, and Knowledge of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza among Poultry Handlers in Selected Live Bird Markets in Nigeria

Authors: Kayode Abraham Olawuyi, Olukayode Olugbenga Orole, Uwem Okon Edet, Foluke Grace Olawuyi, Nicodemus Mkpuma, Chukwudi Ofodile, Clement Meseko
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: The live bird markets (LBMs) are considered a very high-risk area for the transmission of infectious pathogens in poultry due to the large numbers of uninspected mixed poultry species of unknown disease status that are brought together. The objective was to identify gaps in knowledge, risk perception, and biosecurity practices related to highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) LBMs workers in parts of Northern Nigeria. Material and Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted among 226 LBM workers using a structured interviewer-administered questionnaire. Data on socio-demographics, poultry handling practices, biosecurity measures, knowledge, and risk perception were analyzed using descriptive statistics and binary logistic regression. Results: All respondents were male (mean age 40 ± 12.3 years). HPAI awareness was high among LBM workers (>95%); however, knowledge of its zoonotic potential was low ( 90%) of the vendors traded in multiple bird species. Forty-five percent (45%) of the vendors consider the introduction of sick birds into the market to be of low risk, while 42% perceive it as high risk for the transmission of the influenza virus. More than 70% of vendors regularly clean their stalls and cages as an important biosecurity measure. Vendors who separate sick from healthy birds were less likely to experience influenza virus transmission in the market (odds ratio [OR]: 0.214; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.073–0.630). The use of personal protective equipment and disinfection of vehicles used were associated with a reduced risk of transmission of influenza viruses within the LBM (OR: 0.534; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.298–0.953; OR: 0.584; 95% CL: 0.450–0.757), respectively. Conclusion: Despite high awareness, critical gaps in zoonotic knowledge, biosecurity lapses, and low risk perception exist. Targeted risk communication and enhanced biosecurity protocols are urgently needed to mitigate HPAI transmission in these LBMs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the risk of avian influenza transmission in live bird markets by examining knowledge, perception, and biosecurity practices among poultry handlers. The live bird markets are considered a very high-risk area for the transmission of infectious pathogens in poultry due to the large numbers of uninspected mixed poultry species of unknown disease status that are brought together.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 226 LBM workers in parts of Northern Nigeria using a structured interviewer-administered questionnaire. All respondents were male (mean age 40 ± 12.3 years).
Core methods
Data on socio-demographics, poultry handling practices, biosecurity measures, knowledge, and risk perception were analyzed using descriptive statistics and binary logistic regression.
Main results
HPAI awareness was high among LBM workers (>95%); however, knowledge of its zoonotic potential was low. Forty-five percent (45%) of the vendors consider the introduction of sick birds into the market to be of low risk, while 42% perceive it as high risk for the transmission of the influenza virus. Vendors who separate sick from healthy birds were less likely to experience influenza virus transmission in the market (odds ratio [OR]: 0.214; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.073–0.630).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that despite high awareness, critical gaps in zoonotic knowledge, biosecurity lapses, and low risk perception exist. This study highlights the need for targeted risk communication and enhanced biosecurity protocols to mitigate HPAI transmission in these LBMs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted risk communication strategies and the development of enhanced biosecurity protocols to reduce the risk of HPAI transmission in live bird markets, which are critical for public health in Nigeria.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality. The evidence is moderate as it is based on self-reported data and descriptive statistics.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

为什么澳大利亚应紧急为最容易感染 H5N1 禽流感的野生动物接种疫苗

Why Australia should urgently vaccinate wildlife most at risk from H5N1 bird flu
作者： Jane Younger
摘要中文翻译截至撰写本文时,澳大利亚已确认在五个州的 102 只野生海鸟中发现了致命的 H5N1 禽流感,其中包括该国首次出现鸟类大规模死亡事件。问题不再是这种病毒是否会伤害澳大利亚野生动物,而是我们是否应该使用一切可用的工具来保护最危险的物种。疫苗接种 无法阻止 H5N1 通过野生动物传播。但它可以阻止 暴发 杀死一个地区的所有濒危物种——一场悲惨的局部灭绝。澳大利亚应开始针对受威胁鸟类 疫苗接种。它还应该紧急制定针对海豹和海狮的战略,而不是等到数千只海豹和海狮死亡。有政策存在,但行动仍然过于谨慎 澳大利亚有一项国家政策,允许对稀有、受保护和有价值的鸟类进行紧急 疫苗接种,包括动物园、保护区、繁殖计划和一些受管理的野生种群中的动物。政府还购买了灭活的 H5 疫苗(不含活病毒),并正在圈养的非家禽中进行试点研究。这是重要的基础工作,但还不是一个可以与新西兰在 H5N1 到来之前为该国五种最稀有鸟类接种疫苗的雄心勃勃和积极主动的方法相媲美的公共计划。澳大利亚动物园和保护区已获得资金来加强生物安全和圈养“保险种群”。疫苗接种 试点正在进行中,但政府尚未宣布哪些受威胁物种将在何处或何时获得更广泛的 疫苗接种。更重要的是,该政策排除了非鸟类(鸟类)物种,这意味着海洋哺乳动物不被考虑,尽管它们已知容易感染 H5N1。尚未向公众宣布针对澳大利亚 疫苗接种 海豹或海狮试验的计划。有什么海外项目...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
澳大利亚面临着紧迫的公共卫生和保护危机,因为 H5N1 禽流感已在多个州的野生海鸟中造成大规模 死亡率,这引发了一个问题:目标野生动物 死亡率 是否可以防止最脆弱物种的局部灭绝。
设计与对象
该文件是一份政策分析和评论,综合了澳大利亚目前的 H5N1 情况、现有国家政策、疫苗接种 试点工作以及海外计划,以主张对受威胁野生动物采取紧急、有针对性的 疫苗接种。
核心方法
该分析借鉴了 H5N1 检测和 Z​​XQTERMQQXZ 的政府报告、紧急情况 疫苗接种 的政策文件以及已发布的海外 疫苗接种 在鸟类和海豹身上进行试验的记录,以评估可行性和差距。
主要结果
澳大利亚已在五个州的 102 只野生海鸟中确认了 H5N1 病毒,并记录到 2025 年赫德岛估计有 13,359 只海鸟幼崽死亡,其中 5 种鸟类和几种海洋哺乳动物被确定为处于极端或高风险中。
解释与创新
作者解释说,疫苗接种 无法阻止 H5N1 传播,但可以减少严重疾病并防止小规模易接近人群的局部灭绝,这强调需要采取积极主动的针对物种的策略,而不是等待大规模死亡。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持对最危险的野生动物(尤其是受管理环境和优先海豹栖息地中的受威胁鸟类)立即采取有针对性的 疫苗接种,同时将 疫苗接种 与现有的生物安全、监测 和保护措施相结合。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于政策分析和海外病例报告;澳大利亚尚未宣布将在哪些物种、地点或时间接种疫苗,大规模野生动物 疫苗接种 在后勤方面仍然具有挑战性。

Why Australia should urgently vaccinate wildlife most at risk from H5N1 bird flu

Authors: Jane Younger
Original abstract (English)
Australia has confirmed deadly H5N1 bird flu has been found in 102 wild seabirds across five states at the time of writing, including the country’s first mass death of birds. The question is no longer if this virus can harm Australian wildlife, but whether we should use every available tool to protect species most at risk. Vaccination cannot stop H5N1 spreading through wildlife. But it could prevent an outbreak from killing all of the at-risk species in an area – a tragic local extinction. Australia should begin targeted vaccination of threatened birds. It should also urgently develop a strategy for seals and sea lions, rather than waiting until thousands are dead. A policy exists, but action remains too cautious Australia has a national policy that allows emergency vaccination of rare, protected and valuable birds, including animals in zoos, sanctuaries, breeding programs and some managed wild populations. The government has also purchased an inactivated H5 vaccine – containing no live virus – and is running pilot studies in captive non-poultry birds. This is important groundwork, but not yet a public program comparable with New Zealand’s ambitious and proactive approach to vaccinate five of the country’s rarest birds before the arrival of H5N1. Australian zoos and sanctuaries have received funding to strengthen biosecurity and captive “insurance populations”. Pilot vaccinations are underway, but the government has not announced which threatened species will receive wider vaccination, where, or when. More importantly, the policy excludes non-avian (bird) species, meaning marine mammals are not considered despite their known vulnerability to H5N1. No plans have been announced to the public for Australian vaccination trials for seals or sea lions. What overseas programs...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Australia faces an urgent public-health and conservation crisis as H5N1 bird flu has caused mass mortality in wild seabirds across multiple states, raising the question whether targeted wildlife vaccination can prevent local extinctions of the most vulnerable species.
Design and population
The paper is a policy analysis and commentary that synthesizes Australia’s current H5N1 situation, existing national policy, pilot vaccination efforts, and overseas programs to argue for urgent, targeted vaccination of threatened wildlife.
Core methods
The analysis draws on government reports of H5N1 detections and mortality, policy documents on emergency vaccination, and published accounts of overseas vaccination trials in birds and seals to assess feasibility and gaps.
Main results
Australia has confirmed H5N1 in 102 wild seabirds across five states and recorded an estimated 13,359 pup deaths at Heard Island in 2025, with five bird species and several marine mammals identified as at extreme or high risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The author interprets that vaccination cannot stop H5N1 spread but could reduce severe disease and prevent local extinctions in small, accessible populations, highlighting the need for proactive, species-specific strategies rather than waiting for mass die-offs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support immediate, targeted vaccination of the most at-risk wildlife—especially threatened birds in managed settings and priority seal colonies—while integrating vaccination with existing biosecurity, surveillance, and conservation measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to policy analysis and overseas case reports; Australia has not yet announced which species will be vaccinated, where, or when, and broadscale wildlife vaccination remains logistically challenging.

H3N2 犬流感病毒细胞和卵优先复制的遗传决定因素。

Genetic determinants of cell- and egg-preferred replication in H3N2 canine influenza virus.
作者： Rang Wang, Yaohui Zhu, Min Zhu, Xiaolong Li, Huabo Zhou, Linying Peng, Shiqi Li, Haodong Zhang, Shanshan Dong, Yifeng Qin
摘要中文翻译H3N2 犬流感病毒 (CIV) 起源于 禽流感 病毒,已在狗中传播了二十多年,但对其 宿主 适应机制和细胞趋向性仍知之甚少。在这项研究中,我们从犬呼吸道疾病 暴发 中分离出两种表型不同的 H3N2 CIV 毒株(CX3 和 CX12)。尽管两者都是使用 MDCK 细胞分离的,但 CX3 在 MDCK 细胞中表现出更高的复制效率和噬斑形成能力,而 CX12 在鸡胚胎中复制效率更高。利用反向遗传学,我们确定了 PB1 基因是病毒聚合酶活性的关键增强子,而 HA 基因是病毒稳定性的关键贡献者,这两者共同介导细胞偏好的复制。这些发现为 H3N2 CIV 宿主 适应提供了机制见解,并为未来的 疫苗 开发奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解H3N2犬流感病毒的宿主适应机制和细胞向性,该病毒起源于禽流感病毒,已在狗中传播了二十多年,但在这些方面仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,从犬呼吸道疾病 暴发 中分离出两种表型不同的 H3N2 CIV 毒株(CX3 和 CX12)。
核心方法
研究人员利用反向遗传学,确定了 PB1 基因是病毒聚合酶活性的关键增强子,而 HA 基因是病毒稳定性的关键贡献者,以研究它们在病毒复制中的作用。
主要结果
CX3 在 MDCK 细胞中表现出更高的复制效率和噬斑形成能力,而 CX12 在鸡胚中复制效率更高,其中 PB1 和 HA 基因共同介导细胞偏好的复制。
解释与创新
这些发现为 H3N2 CIV 宿主 适应提供了机制见解,并为未来的 疫苗 开发奠定了基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解病毒的趋向性和适应性,这可能为犬流感病毒的 监测 策略和 疫苗 设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于有限菌株的体外实验,由于调查范围狭窄,证据强度中等。

Genetic determinants of cell- and egg-preferred replication in H3N2 canine influenza virus.

Authors: Rang Wang, Yaohui Zhu, Min Zhu, Xiaolong Li, Huabo Zhou, Linying Peng, Shiqi Li, Haodong Zhang, Shanshan Dong, Yifeng Qin
Original abstract (English)
The H3N2 canine influenza virus (CIV), which originated from avian influenza viruses and has circulated in dogs for over two decades, remains poorly understood in terms of its host adaptation mechanisms and cellular tropism. In this study, we isolated two phenotypically distinct H3N2 CIV strains (CX3 and CX12) from a canine respiratory disease outbreak. Although both were isolated using MDCK cells, CX3 showed higher replication efficiency and plaque-forming ability in MDCK cells, whereas CX12 replicated more efficiently in chicken embryos. Using reverse genetics, we identified the PB1 gene as a critical enhancer of viral polymerase activity and the HA gene as a key contributor to viral stability, both of which collectively mediate cell-preferred replication. These findings provide mechanistic insights into H3N2 CIV host adaptation and offer a foundation for future vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the host adaptation mechanisms and cellular tropism of H3N2 canine influenza virus, which originated from avian influenza viruses and has circulated in dogs for over two decades but remains poorly understood in these aspects.
Design and population
In this study, two phenotypically distinct H3N2 CIV strains (CX3 and CX12) were isolated from a canine respiratory disease outbreak.
Core methods
Using reverse genetics, the researchers identified the PB1 gene as a critical enhancer of viral polymerase activity and the HA gene as a key contributor to viral stability to investigate their roles in viral replication.
Main results
CX3 showed higher replication efficiency and plaque-forming ability in MDCK cells, whereas CX12 replicated more efficiently in chicken embryos, with the PB1 and HA genes collectively mediating cell-preferred replication.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide mechanistic insights into H3N2 CIV host adaptation and offer a foundation for future vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results contribute to understanding viral tropism and adaptation, which may inform surveillance strategies and vaccine design for canine influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro experiments with limited strains, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the narrow scope of the investigation.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

流行病学,2020-2024 年柬埔寨严重急性呼吸道感染患者流感和 SARS-CoV-2 阳性的趋势和决定因素:基于医院的回顾性横断面 监测 数据分析。

Epidemiology, trends and determinants of influenza and SARS-CoV-2 positivity among severe acute respiratory infection patients in Cambodia, 2020-2024: a hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis.
作者： Khemrin Pong, Hayputhik Long, Sokly Mom, Sophanith Ung, Darapheak Chau, Sengdoeurn Yi, Piseth Keam, Heng Seng, Ly Sovann
摘要中文翻译目的:描述柬埔寨住院的严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)患者中流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的流行病学模式、时间趋势以及确诊的严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)阳性的决定因素。设计:使用 2020 年至 2024 年收集的国家哨点 SARI 监测 数据进行基于医院的回顾性横断面 监测 数据分析。背景:9 家 SARI 哨点医院位于柬埔寨各地。参与者:共有 16 739 名符合 SARI 病例定义的住院患者。衡量标准:SARI 确诊阳性将所有实验室确诊的流感或 SARS-CoV-2 感染的 SARI 病例定义为主要结果指标。进行描述性和双变量分析,然后进行多变量逻辑回归以确定与 SARI 确认的阳性相关的因素。结果:16 739例SARI病例中,中位年龄为16岁(IQR=59),其中男性占54.6%;13.5% 经实验室确诊感染流感或 SARS-CoV-2。2021 年 死亡率 发病率较高。2020 年,A(H3N2) 占主导地位,随后 2021 年 SARS-CoV-2 广泛传播。2022 年,SARS-CoV-2 与 A(H3N2) 和 B 型流感(维多利亚)共同传播。随着 SARS-CoV-2 和 A(H3N2) 的减少,A(H1N1)pdm 成为 2023 年的主要毒株。2024年,A(H3N2)再次引领流通,同时检测到B(维多利亚)和SARS-CoV-2。散发性 A(H5N1) 病例于 2023 年底开始出现。多变量分析表明,与 5-14 岁儿童(调整后 OR (aOR)=1.85;95% CI 1.47 至 2.31)和 50-64 岁成人(aOR=1.36;95% CI 1.15 至 1.60)相比,SARI 确诊阳性的几率更高与其他地区相比,25-49 岁成年人、雨季期间(aOR=2.09;95% CI 1.89 至 2.32)以及金边患者中(p<0.001)。继 COVID-19 大流行期间观察到流感检测下降后,2022 年至 2024 年流感检测显着增加。结论:这些结果表明,各时期 SARI 趋势有所增加,每年流行的流感亚型也发生变化,并且在柬埔寨 Delta 变种 COVID-19 大流行期间 死亡率 率较高。年龄、地区、年份和季节是与 SARI 确诊阳性相关的重要因素。应优先针对特定高危人群采取有针对性的干预和预防措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述柬埔寨住院的 SARI 患者的流行病学模式、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的时间趋势以及严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 确诊阳性的决定因素。
设计与对象
利用2020年至2024年收集的国家哨点SARI 监测数据进行了以医院为基础的回顾性横断面监测数据分析,涉及柬埔寨全国9家SARI哨点医院以及总共16,739名符合SARI病例定义的住院患者。
核心方法
进行描述性和双变量分析,然后进行多变量逻辑回归以确定与 SARI 确认的阳性相关的因素。
主要结果
在 16,739 例 SARI 病例中,13.5% 经实验室确诊为流感或 SARS-CoV-2。多变量分析表明,雨季期间,与 25-49 岁成人相比,5-14 岁儿童(调整后 OR (aOR)=1.85;95% CI 1.47 至 2.31)和 50-64 岁成人(aOR=1.36;95% CI 1.15 至 1.60)SARI 确诊阳性的几率更高(aOR=2.09;95% CI 1.89 至 2.32)以及金边患者与其他地区的患者相比(p<0.001)。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了每年流行的流感亚型的变化以及与 SARI 确诊阳性相关的重要因素,包括年龄、地区、年份和季节。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为雨季期间和金边的特定高危群体,特别是 50-64 岁的儿童和成人提供有针对性的干预和预防措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对 监测 数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差并限制研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Epidemiology, trends and determinants of influenza and SARS-CoV-2 positivity among severe acute respiratory infection patients in Cambodia, 2020-2024: a hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis.

Authors: Khemrin Pong, Hayputhik Long, Sokly Mom, Sophanith Ung, Darapheak Chau, Sengdoeurn Yi, Piseth Keam, Heng Seng, Ly Sovann
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To describe the epidemiological pattern, temporal trends of influenza and SARS-CoV-2, and determinants of severe acute respiratory infection (SARI)-confirmed positivity among patients hospitalised with SARI in Cambodia. DESIGN: A hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis using national sentinel SARI surveillance data collected from 2020 to 2024. SETTING: Nine SARI sentinel hospitals are located across Cambodia. PARTICIPANTS: A total of 16 739 hospitalised patients who met the SARI case definition. MEASURE: SARI-confirmed positivity defines all SARI cases with laboratory-confirmed influenza or SARS-CoV-2 infections as the main outcome measure. Descriptive and bivariate analyses were performed, followed by multivariate logistic regression to identify factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity. RESULTS: Among 16 739 SARI cases, median age in years was 16 (IQR=59), with 54.6% being male; 13.5% were laboratory-confirmed for influenza or SARS-CoV-2. High mortality rate in 2021. A(H3N2) dominated in 2020, followed by widespread SARS-CoV-2 circulation in 2021. In 2022, SARS-CoV-2 co-circulated with A(H3N2) and influenza B (Victoria). A(H1N1)pdm became the predominant strain in 2023 as SARS-CoV-2 and A(H3N2) declined. In 2024, A(H3N2) again led circulation, with B(Victoria) and SARS-CoV-2 also detected. Sporadic A(H5N1) cases began appearing in late 2023. Multivariable analysis indicated higher odds of SARI-confirmed positivity among children aged 5-14 years (adjusted OR (aOR)=1.85; 95% CI 1.47 to 2.31) and adults 50-64 years (aOR=1.36; 95% CI 1.15 to 1.60) compared with adults 25-49 years, during the rainy season (aOR=2.09; 95% CI 1.89 to 2.32) and among patients in Phnom Penh compared with other regions (p<0.001). Influenza detection markedly increased from 2022 to 2024 following the decline observed during the COVID-19 pandemic. CONCLUSION: These results indicate that there was an increase in SARI trends over the periods and the changing influenza subtypes circulating each year, along with a high mortality rate during the Delta variant COVID-19 pandemic in Cambodia. Age, region, year and season were significant factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity. Targeted interventions and preventive measures should be prioritised for specific high-risk groups.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the epidemiological pattern, temporal trends of influenza and SARS-CoV-2, and determinants of severe acute respiratory infection (SARI)-confirmed positivity among patients hospitalised with SARI in Cambodia.
Design and population
A hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis was conducted using national sentinel SARI surveillance data collected from 2020 to 2024, involving nine SARI sentinel hospitals across Cambodia and a total of 16,739 hospitalised patients who met the SARI case definition.
Core methods
Descriptive and bivariate analyses were performed, followed by multivariate logistic regression to identify factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity.
Main results
Among 16,739 SARI cases, 13.5% were laboratory-confirmed for influenza or SARS-CoV-2. Multivariable analysis indicated higher odds of SARI-confirmed positivity among children aged 5-14 years (adjusted OR (aOR)=1.85; 95% CI 1.47 to 2.31) and adults 50-64 years (aOR=1.36; 95% CI 1.15 to 1.60) compared with adults 25-49 years, during the rainy season (aOR=2.09; 95% CI 1.89 to 2.32) and among patients in Phnom Penh compared with other regions (p<0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the changing influenza subtypes circulating each year and the significant factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity, including age, region, year and season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform targeted interventions and preventive measures for specific high-risk groups, particularly children and adults aged 50-64 years, during the rainy season and in Phnom Penh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on surveillance data, which may introduce bias and limit the generalisability of findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

中和性和保护性单克隆抗体可识别 H7 型流感血凝素中的抗原位点,并选择全球范围内很少观察到的氨基酸取代。

Neutralizing and protective monoclonal antibodies identify antigenic sites in influenza H7 hemagglutinin and select for amino acid substitutions rarely observed at the global level.
作者： Gabriel I Parra, Marcus Odin, Kentaro Tohma, Falko Schmeisser, Clement A Meseda, Wei Wang, Carol D Weiss, Russell Vassell, Amy Woerner, Cyntia L Pedro
摘要中文翻译H7 亚型的 禽流感 病毒可感染多种鸟类和哺乳动物,更好地了解 H7 病毒抗原性和免疫原性对于针对潜在的 H7 大流行威胁制定有效对策至关重要。我们开发了针对 H7 血凝素 (HA) 的多种鼠单克隆抗体 (mAb),并表征了这些 mAb 所结合的 HA 表位、它们针对多种 H7 毒株的体外中和活性,以及​​它们在体内提供针对病毒攻击的保护的能力。大多数分离的 mAb 识别流感 HA 球状头部上的抗原位点 A 和 B,但 mAb 也分离到 HA 上的非规范位点。针对 mAb 生成的逃逸病毒用于进一步定义 H7 HA 上的抗原位点,值得注意的是,我们发现这些逃逸病毒中发现的大多数氨基酸取代在全球水平上都是罕见的。最后,我们使用病毒感染产生的多克隆抗体来评估抗体结合和中和是否受到单克隆抗体产生的逃逸病毒 HA 中单个氨基酸取代的影响。结果表明,所有逃逸病毒(每种病毒均携带单个氨基酸取代)仍可被多克隆抗体有效中和,这表明对 H7 感染的抗体反应是广泛且冗余的。重要性 H7 流感病毒感染多种鸟类和哺乳动物物种,并对人类健康构成潜在威胁。我们使用一组鼠单克隆抗体 (mAb) 来促进 H7 血凝素 (HA) 上重要保护性表位的识别。虽然大多数抗体与 H7 HA 上的抗原位点 A 和 B 结合,这些抗原位点对应于 H3 流感病毒上已明确表征的同源位点,但一些抗体似乎可以识别 HA 中不同于那些已知抗原位点的新表位。我们还鉴定了这些 mAb 选择的逃逸病毒中的氨基酸取代,并评估了它们在超过 60 年的时间里对多克隆反应和天然 H7 病毒 进化 的影响。总而言之,这些结果增加了我们对 H7 流感病毒抗原性和免疫原性的理解,并可能对 H7 疫苗 的成功开发产生积极影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了更好地了解 H7 禽流感 病毒的抗原性和免疫原性的需要,以便为大流行准备和针对潜在 H7 大流行威胁的 疫苗 开发提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一组针对 H7 血凝素 (HA) 的鼠单克隆抗体 (mAb),并在体外和体内表征了它们对 H7 菌株的结合、中和活性和保护能力。
核心方法
分离鼠单克隆抗体并定位到 H7 HA 上的抗原位点,包括典型位点 A 和 B 以及非典型位点。产生逃逸病毒是为了识别影响抗体结合的氨基酸取代,并使用病毒感染的多克隆抗体评估其影响。
主要结果
HA 中携带单个氨基酸取代的逃逸病毒可被多克隆抗体有效中和,表明对 H7 感染有广泛且冗余的抗体反应。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,mAb 选择的大多数氨基酸取代在全球范围内都是罕见的,这表明天然 H7 进化 可能不容易逃避多克隆反应,这对于 H7 抗原性图谱来说是新颖的。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以通过识别保守的保护性表位并指导选择最大限度地减少逃逸可能性的抗原来为 H7 疫苗 设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于鼠类单克隆抗体和体外/体内模型,这些模型可能无法完全概括人类 免疫应答,从而限制了对人类感染的普遍性。

Neutralizing and protective monoclonal antibodies identify antigenic sites in influenza H7 hemagglutinin and select for amino acid substitutions rarely observed at the global level.

Authors: Gabriel I Parra, Marcus Odin, Kentaro Tohma, Falko Schmeisser, Clement A Meseda, Wei Wang, Carol D Weiss, Russell Vassell, Amy Woerner, Cyntia L Pedro
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza viruses of the H7 subtype infect a variety of avian as well as mammalian species, and a better understanding of H7 virus antigenicity and immunogenicity is vital to the development of effective countermeasures for a potential H7 pandemic threat. We have developed a diverse panel of murine monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) to the H7 hemagglutinin (HA) and characterized these mAbs for the HA epitope(s) that they bind, their neutralizing activity in vitro against several strains of H7, and their ability to provide protection against virus challenge in vivo . The majority of the isolated mAbs recognized antigenic sites A and B on the globular head of the influenza HA, but mAbs were also isolated to non-canonical sites on HA. Escape viruses generated against the mAbs were used to further define the antigenic sites on the H7 HA, and notably, we find that most amino acid substitutions identified in these escape viruses are rare at the global level. Finally, we used a polyclonal antibody generated by virus infection to assess whether antibody binding and neutralization were impacted by individual amino acid substitutions in the HA of escape viruses generated by the mAbs. The results showed that all of the escape viruses, each of which carried a single amino acid substitution, were still effectively neutralized by polyclonal antibodies, suggesting that the antibody response to H7 infection was broad and redundant.IMPORTANCEInfluenza H7 viruses infect a variety of avian and mammalian species, and present a potential threat to human health. We used a panel of murine monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) to facilitate the identification of important protective epitopes on the H7 hemagglutinin (HA). While most of the antibodies bind to antigenic sites A and B on the H7 HA that correspond to well-characterized cognate sites on influenza H3, some antibodies appear to recognize new epitopes in HA distinct from those known antigenic sites. We also identified amino acid substitutions in escape viruses selected by these mAbs and evaluated their impact in the context of polyclonal responses and natural H7 virus evolution over a >60-year period. Taken together, the results add to our understanding of influenza H7 virus antigenicity and immunogenicity, and may have positive implications for successful H7 vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to better understand the antigenicity and immunogenicity of H7 avian influenza viruses to inform pandemic preparedness and vaccine development against a potential H7 pandemic threat.
Design and population
The study developed a panel of murine monoclonal antibodies (mAbs) targeting the H7 hemagglutinin (HA) and characterized their binding, neutralizing activity, and protective capacity against H7 strains in vitro and in vivo.
Core methods
Murine monoclonal antibodies were isolated and mapped to antigenic sites on the H7 HA, including canonical sites A and B and non-canonical sites. Escape viruses were generated to identify amino acid substitutions affecting antibody binding, and their impact was assessed using polyclonal antibodies from virus infection.
Main results
Escape viruses carrying single amino acid substitutions in HA were effectively neutralized by polyclonal antibodies, indicating a broad and redundant antibody response to H7 infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that most amino acid substitutions selected by mAbs are rare globally, suggesting that natural H7 evolution may not readily evade polyclonal responses, which is novel for H7 antigenicity mapping.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results may inform H7 vaccine design by identifying conserved protective epitopes and guiding the selection of antigens that minimize escape potential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on murine mAbs and in vitro/in vivo models, which may not fully recapitulate human immune responses, limiting generalizability to human infections.

美国流感中人与动物界面的一个健康盲点 监测。

The One Health Blind Spot at the Human‒Animal Interface in US Influenza Surveillance.
作者： Cole King, A Paige Adams
摘要中文翻译新型甲型流感病毒在 21 世纪造成了数千人的生命和数十亿美元的经济损失,过去 4 次大流行甲型流感病毒中有 2 次可能起源于北美大陆的动物种群。尽管如此,美国的人类和兽用流感 监测 系统连接较差,并且未达到国会对数据共享和互操作性的要求。这使美国处于一种被动的姿态,重点是遏制已经发生的暴发,而不是积极主动的暴发和预防。COVID-19 大流行推动了对 监测 基础设施和基因组技术的重大投资,以及新的国家统一健康框架,为实现流感 监测 现代化提供了独特的机会,同时更加强调人类和动物健康的相互联系。然而,随着大流行时代资金的退出,增强型 监测 的资金将大幅减少。对新型 监测 技术的持续投资对于解决最近的 H5N1 流感 暴发 至关重要,对于防止未来的经济损失以及人类和动物生命的损失也至关重要。(《美国公共卫生杂志》。2026;116(8):1220-1228。https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308445)。

The One Health Blind Spot at the Human‒Animal Interface in US Influenza Surveillance.

Authors: Cole King, A Paige Adams
Original abstract (English)
Novel influenza A viruses have cost thousands of lives and billions of dollars of economic losses in the 21st century, and 2 of the past 4 pandemic influenza A viruses likely originated in animal populations on the North American continent. Despite this, the human and veterinary influenza surveillance systems in the United States are poorly connected and have fallen short of congressional mandates for data sharing and interoperability. This places the United States in a reactive posture that is focused on containment of outbreaks that have already occurred rather than proactive surveillance and prevention. The significant investments in surveillance infrastructure and genomic technologies spurred by the COVID-19 pandemic, along with the new National One Health Framework, present a unique opportunity to modernize influenza surveillance with a greater emphasis on the interconnectedness of human and animal health. However, funding for enhanced surveillance is set to decrease significantly as pandemic-era funding sunsets. Continuing investments into novel surveillance technologies have been critical in addressing the recent H5N1 influenza outbreaks and will be critical to prevent future economic losses and losses of both human and animal life. ( Am J Public Health . 2026;116(8):1220-1228. https://doi.org/10.2105/AJPH.2026.308445).

基于 GIS 的 One-Health 监测 标准化环境特征框架:湿地高致病性禽流感监测案例研究

A GIS-based framework for standardized environmental characterization in One-Health surveillance: a case study of HPAI monitoring in wetlands
作者： De Conti G, Macario R, Martelli L, Fornasiero D, Mulatti P, Panzarin V, Mazzucato M
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 由分支 2.3.4.4b 的 H5 病毒引起的 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 动物疫病在时空传播和 宿主 谱方面不断重塑疾病生态。因此,越来越需要用将环境因素纳入流行病学解释的方法来补充传统的 监测 方法。尽管环境 监测 在“同一个健康”框架内得到越来越多的认可,但环境描述符的结构并不总是一致,这强调了采用标准化方法进行收集和管理的重要性。方法 开发了基于 GIS 的框架来标准化 HPAI 监测 场地的环境特征。使用 ESRI 现场地图工具收集意大利东北部的九个湿地的环境数据,并通过 ArcGIS Online 同步。数据通过实地地面实况验证,存储在空间关系数据库中,并汇总成统一的特定地点描述符,适合流行病学建模和分析。结果本研究引入了一个基于 GIS 的框架,旨在标准化 HPAI 监测 采样点环境特征的记录方式。该工作流程将不同类型的现场观察转化为可分析的有组织的地理数据集。当在意大利东北部的湿地 监测 站点使用时,该框架为每个站点生成了一致的环境数据和标准化记录。结论 该工作流程结合了移动 GIS 工具(ESRI Field Maps)、ArcGIS Online/Pro 和 PostGIS 数据库,为流行病学分析创建标准化输出。这种设置在野外生态学、空间数据基础设施和疾病风险分析之间建立了可靠的联系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了将环境因素纳入对由 H5 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒引起的 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 动物流行病学解释的需要,该病毒正在传播和 宿主 范围方面重塑疾病生态学。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一个基于 GIS 的框架,以标准化 HPAI 监测 站点的环境特征,使用通过 ArcGIS Online 同步的 ESRI 现场地图工具收集意大利东北部九个湿地的数据。
核心方法
使用 ESRI 现场地图工具收集环境数据,通过基于现场的地面实况进行验证,存储在空间关系数据库 (PostGIS) 中,并汇总为适合流行病学建模的统一的特定地点描述符。
主要结果
该工作流程为意大利东北部的九个湿地 监测 站点中的每一个站点生成了一致的环境数据和标准化记录,从而能够组织有组织的地理数据集以进行分析。
解释与创新
作者将该框架解释为野外生态学、空间数据基础设施和疾病风险分析之间的可靠联系,在 One Health 框架内引入了一种标准化的环境 监测 方法。
科研与公卫意义
这种基于 GIS 的框架可以通过提供支持湿地生态系统流行病学解释和风险评估的标准化环境描述符来改进 HPAI 监测。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象级别的报告,缺乏对该框架对 监测 结果或疾病风险预测影响的详细验证,因此需要进行低置信度评估。

A GIS-based framework for standardized environmental characterization in One-Health surveillance: a case study of HPAI monitoring in wetlands

Authors: De Conti G, Macario R, Martelli L, Fornasiero D, Mulatti P, Panzarin V, Mazzucato M
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) panzootic caused by H5 viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b is constantly re-shaping disease ecology in terms of spatio-temporal spread and host spectrum. Because of this, there is an increasing need to complement traditional surveillance methods with approaches that consider environmental factors into epidemiological interpretation. Although environmental surveillance is increasingly recognized within a One Health framework, environmental descriptors are not always consistently structured, underscoring the importance of adopting standardized approaches for their collection and management. Method A GIS-based framework was developed to standardize environmental characterization of HPAI surveillance sites. Environmental data were collected at nine wetlands in north-eastern Italy, using ESRI field map tool, synchronised through ArcGIS online. Data were validated with field-base ground truthing, stored in a spatial relational database and aggregated into harmonised site-specific descriptors, suitable for epidemiological modelling and analyses. Results This study introduces a GIS-based framework designed to standardize how environmental features are recorded at sampling sites for HPAI surveillance. The workflow turns different types of field observations into organized geographical datasets that can be analysed. When used at wetland surveillance sites in north-eastern Italy, this framework produced consistent environmental data and standardized records for each site. Conclusions The workflow combines mobile GIS tools (ESRI Field Maps), ArcGIS Online/Pro, and a PostGIS database to create standardized outputs for epidemiological analysis. This setup creates a reliable connection between field ecology, spatial data infrastructures, and disease risk analysis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to integrate environmental factors into epidemiological interpretation of the Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) panzootic caused by H5 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses, which is reshaping disease ecology in terms of spread and host range.
Design and population
The research developed a GIS-based framework to standardize environmental characterization of HPAI surveillance sites, collecting data at nine wetlands in north-eastern Italy using ESRI Field Map tools synchronized through ArcGIS Online.
Core methods
Environmental data were collected using ESRI Field Map tools, validated with field-based ground truthing, stored in a spatial relational database (PostGIS), and aggregated into harmonized site-specific descriptors suitable for epidemiological modelling.
Main results
The workflow produced consistent environmental data and standardized records for each of the nine wetland surveillance sites in north-eastern Italy, enabling organized geographical datasets for analysis.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the framework as a reliable connection between field ecology, spatial data infrastructures, and disease risk analysis, introducing a standardized approach to environmental surveillance within a One Health framework.
Scientific and public-health significance
This GIS-based framework can improve HPAI surveillance by providing standardized environmental descriptors that support epidemiological interpretation and risk assessment in wetland ecosystems.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level reporting and lacks detailed validation of the framework's impact on surveillance outcomes or disease risk prediction, warranting a low confidence assessment.

巴西马拉尼奥野生动物与家禽交汇处的自给家禽系统中的生物安全实践和风险感知

BIOSECURITY PRACTICES AND RISK PERCEPTION IN SUBSISTENCE POULTRY SYSTEMS AT THE WILDLIFE–DOMESTIC INTERFACE IN MARANHÃO, BRAZIL
作者： Jessica Stefane Costa Hammes, Cinthya Letícia Correa Belfort, Assuero Batista Feitosa Júnior, Viviane Correa Silva Coimbra, Carla Janaína Rebouças Marques do Rosário
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 对动物健康、公共卫生和粮食安全构成重大威胁,特别是在迁徙 高致病性禽流感 和自给家禽系统之间存在接口的地区。在这种情况下,生物安全薄弱以及获得信息和兽医服务的机会有限可能会增加病毒引入和传播的风险。本研究的目的是评估巴西马拉尼昂州帕纳夸提拉迁徙地周围自给家禽系统的管理和生物安全实践、风险认知和预防行动的接受度。2025 年 1 月至 6 月期间,通过对 70 名家禽饲养者应用结构化问卷,进行了一项横断面描述性研究。分析的变量包括社会经济概况、鸡群特征、卫生习惯、有关候鸟和禽流感的知识以及对教育策略的兴趣。使用描述性统计和探索性关联分析来分析数据。观察到,正规教育程度较低的饲养员占主导地位,同时多物种鸡群主要用于自我消费,且基本生物安全措施的采用有限。尽管许多受访者表示认识到鸟类患病的迹象,但所采取的行动主要基于个人经验,对兽医援助的需求较低,而且向官方服务部门发出的通知也有限。关于候鸟流行病学作用的知识有限。相反,人们对技术指导和卫生教育举措的接受度很高。结果表明,位于生态敏感地区的生计系统中存在高致病性禽流感预防的相关脆弱性,但也强调了通过适合当地情况的教育干预措施加强参与性 监测 和预防的机会。

观察到,正规教育程度较低的饲养员占主导地位,同时多物种鸡群主要用于自我消费,且基本生物安全措施的采用有限。

BIOSECURITY PRACTICES AND RISK PERCEPTION IN SUBSISTENCE POULTRY SYSTEMS AT THE WILDLIFE–DOMESTIC INTERFACE IN MARANHÃO, BRAZIL

Authors: Jessica Stefane Costa Hammes, Cinthya Letícia Correa Belfort, Assuero Batista Feitosa Júnior, Viviane Correa Silva Coimbra, Carla Janaína Rebouças Marques do Rosário
Original abstract (English)
Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) represents a significant threat to animal health, public health, and food security, particularly in areas where there is an interface between migratory wild birds and subsistence domestic poultry systems. Under such conditions, weaknesses in biosecurity and limited access to information and veterinary services may increase the risk of virus introduction and dissemination. The objective of this study was to evaluate management and biosecurity practices, risk perception, and receptiveness to preventive actions in subsistence poultry systems located around the migratory site of Panaquatira, Maranhão, Brazil. A cross-sectional descriptive study was conducted between January and June 2025 through the application of a structured questionnaire to 70 poultry keepers. The analyzed variables included socioeconomic profile, flock characteristics, sanitary practices, knowledge about migratory birds and avian influenza, and interest in educational strategies. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and exploratory association analyses. A predominance of keepers with low formal education was observed, along with multispecies flocks mainly intended for self-consumption and limited adoption of basic biosecurity measures. Although many respondents reported recognizing signs of illness in birds, the actions taken were primarily based on personal experience, with low demand for veterinary assistance and limited notification to official services. Knowledge regarding the epidemiological role of migratory birds was restricted. Conversely, a high level of receptiveness to technical guidance and sanitary education initiatives was observed. The results indicate the existence of relevant vulnerabilities for HPAI prevention in subsistence systems located in ecologically sensitive areas, but also highlight opportunities to strengthen participatory surveillance and prevention through educational interventions adapted to the local context.
